# University & education-only programs (.edu / student email)

*23 providers · 522 verified findings · as of 2026-10-22*

**Mapping summary:** Verification pass run 2026-10-22 against official pages. Verified this run: Azure for Students, GCP free/education, Unity, Discord UNiDAYS offer, JetBrains, MATLAB, Zoho, AWS Free Plan, Adobe, Claude for Startups. The session's shared WebSearch budget was exhausted mid-run, so remaining items are compiled from known programs and marked 'verify' where limits may have shifted. Major 2025-2026 changes flagged in notes (Azure $100 lump-sum credit; Unity $200K threshold; Adobe 74%; AWS Free Plan revamp Oct 2025; Discord has no permanent student discount; edX sunset into Google Cloud; JetBrains bootcamp sunset Oct 1 2026; Zoho Catalyst free for students Sept 2026; Google for Startups raised to $350K).

### Adobe Creative Cloud for Students

- **Adobe Express Free Plan** (high) — Free tier of Adobe Express, no expiration, no credit card required. Includes 100,000+ templates, 1M+ royalty-free Adobe Stock assets, 4,000+ fonts, standard photo/video/document editing, 5 GB cloud storage, 10-day version history, 10 PDF Quick Actions/month, videos up to 30 min, and limited free generative AI: 'free daily generations' for insert/remove objects, 5 lifetime uses of Generate Presentations, and 2 lifetime uses of 5-second video generation. Export limited to JPEG/PNG/PDF/GIF/PPT (no transparent-background PNG). Paid Premium is US$9.99/mo (30-day free trial) with 250 monthly generative credits, 100 GB storage, 4K export, 3 accounts per social network.
  - *Eligibility:* Anyone with a free Adobe account; no .edu email, no student status, no credit card required. Available worldwide (page shown in US locale; region-specific bundles exist, e.g., Airtel India, IM3 Indonesia). K-12 US educators can create free classroom accounts via Google Workspace for Education.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Go to adobe.com/express 2) Sign up with a free Adobe account (email or social sign-in) 3) Use Express web/app free; do not select the Premium plan. Students may already have free Express Premium via school .edu login (per page FAQ).
  - *Expiry:* No expiry; free tier has no time limit. Trial of Premium is 30 days; trial converts to paid (US$9.99/mo) unless cancelled.
  - *Gotchas:* Generative AI on the free tier is heavily capped (lifetime uses, not monthly) and 'free daily generations' are standard-model only; 250 monthly Firefly credits require the US$9.99/mo Premium plan. Free tier has no 4K export, no transparent PNGs, 5 GB storage cap.
  - *Source:* https://www.adobe.com/express/pricing.html
- **Student Spaces in Acrobat (AI study tools, free)** (medium) — Adobe's free AI study feature: Student Spaces turns notes into flashcards and quizzes ('an AI study buddy'). The official landing page states 'Get started for free'. Listed on Adobe's education page among 'several free tools students can start using right away', alongside Adobe Express and Firefly. No Creative Cloud subscription is required.
  - *Eligibility:* Any student with a free Adobe account; no .edu email requirement stated on the page. No credit card required.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Sign in at acrobat.adobe.com/studyspace/collection/create-new-project (link from adobe.com/education.html) 2) Upload notes or a document 3) Generate flashcards/quizzes in the free Student Spaces project.
  - *Expiry:* Ongoing, no published sunset date. Some advanced Acrobat features remain in paid Acrobat Studio/Pro plans; Student Spaces itself is free to start.
  - *Gotchas:* Landing page is thin on detail (title/headline only); confirm the full feature set (e.g., AI Assistant quotas, PDF import limits) in-app. Adobe's page copy ties it to Acrobat, so it may nudge toward paid Acrobat plans for heavy document workflows.
  - *Source:* https://acrobat.adobe.com/studyspace/
- **7-day free trial of Creative Cloud Pro (incl. Firefly)** (high) — Full 7-day trial of Creative Cloud Pro: 20+ apps (Photoshop, Illustrator, Premiere, After Effects, Lightroom, Acrobat, Firefly, Express, Fresco) on desktop and iPad. Payment method required at signup but you are not charged until the trial ends; billing converts automatically to Creative Cloud Pro on Day 8 (regular US$69.99/mo; promo US$34.99/mo for first 3 months, new subscribers). A 14-day money-back window after conversion allows cancellation for a full refund. Desktop suite and iPad app are downloadable during the trial; the trial is NOT available on phones.
  - *Eligibility:* Any Adobe account holder; only ONE 7-day CC Pro trial per person (trial already used = not eligible). Student/teacher front: eligible students can 'start a 7-day free trial' of the student plan before the paid student subscription (adobe.com/education/students/creativecloud.html).
  - *How to claim:* 1) Go to adobe.com/creativecloud/free-trial.html (or the CC plans page) 2) Create/sign in to an Adobe account 3) Enter payment details (no charge until Day 8) 4) Download/install apps 5) To avoid paying: cancel via account page > Manage Plans > Cancel Plan within 7 days; if you miss the window you have 14 days post-trial to cancel for a full refund.
  - *Expiry:* Trial = 7 days, one-time only; refund window = 14 days after trial ends. If not cancelled, plan auto-renews monthly at US$69.99/mo (or promo US$34.99/mo x3 then $69.99).
  - *Gotchas:* Free-trial vs free-tier trap: this is a trial, not a free plan - a card/payment method is required on file and automatic billing starts on Day 8. One trial per person lifetime. Trial unavailable on phones (desktop/iPad only).
  - *Source:* https://www.adobe.com/creativecloud/free-trial.html
- **Creative Cloud Pro for Students & Teachers (discount, not free) - verified at 71% (US) / 75% (EU)** (high) — The discounted education plan: US$19.99/mo for the first year, then US$39.99/mo as long as eligibility continues ('Save 71%' vs US$69.99/mo list). EU listing shows 75% off: 19.66 EUR/mo incl. VAT (first year, annual-billed-monthly) vs 79.30 EUR/mo list; Israeli listing shows NIS 88.99/mo year 1, NIS 179.99/mo year 2+. Includes 20+ apps, unlimited standard generative AI features (e.g., Generative Fill), 4,000 monthly generative credits for premium AI video/audio (e.g., Text-to-Video), Firefly Boards (unlimited), non-Adobe models in Firefly (Adobe, Google, OpenAI), Express Premium, Frame.io, 100 GB cloud storage. A 7-day free trial of the student plan is available first. Note: the '74% off' figure in the brief is not what current pages show - US says 71%, EU says 75%.
  - *Eligibility:* Students: 13+, currently enrolled at an accredited school/college/university or qualified online program; teachers: educators/staff at an accredited institution. Adobe verifies status and may request school-issued email, student/teacher ID, enrollment document, transcript, class schedule, pay slip, or offer letter from the current academic year. Purchase must be made by someone 18+ with a credit card. Renewal requires continuing eligibility (annual re-verification); year-2+ price doubles (US$19.99 -> US$39.99/mo).
  - *How to claim:* 1) adobe.com/education/students/creativecloud.html 2) Select Creative Cloud Pro for Students, start 7-day trial or buy 3) Verify eligibility with school email/ID 4) If your school has a campus license, skip this entirely and use the institutional offer (see next finding). One subscription: sign-in on 2 computers, active use on 1.
  - *Expiry:* Discount lasts as long as eligibility is verified; price rises after year 1 (US$19.99 -> US$39.99/mo). Renewal re-verification required each year.
  - *Gotchas:* This is a paid discount, not free - 'free access' only via school institutional licensing. Year-1 vs year-2 pricing gap is the biggest trap. Requires age 18+ to pay; verification can be requested AFTER purchase. CC required for the full app suite; 100 GB storage does not cover heavy video projects (eject/egress: no public data-egress fees, but storage overage forces pay-as-you-go add-ons).
  - *Source:* https://www.adobe.com/education/students/creativecloud.html
- **Institutional / campus-wide Creative Cloud licensing (free for students at partner schools)** (medium) — Many universities and K-12 districts purchase 'Creative Cloud for education institutions' (20+ apps, plan=edu_inst; pricing by phone/institutional sales). At partner schools, students and faculty typically get the full suite at no personal cost by logging in with their school account; the education page states 'some schools provide free access to the full Creative Cloud suite' and directs students to their IT department. K-12 and higher-ed dedicated pages exist (/education/k12.html, /education/higher-ed.html); higher-ed page emphasizes FERPA/COPPA compliance and 'no training on student content'.
  - *Eligibility:* Current students/faculty at institutions that hold an Adobe institutional or K-12 agreement (varies by country and school). Usually requires a school email/SSO; no personal credit card. No age/funding caps - it's per-institution, not per-startup.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Check your school's portal / IT department / Adobe Admin Console login link 2) Sign in with your institution-issued Adobe account 3) Download CC apps - no purchase, no trial needed. If your school has no deal, fall back to the student discount plan above.
  - *Expiry:* Valid for as long as you are enrolled/employed at the institution; access ends at graduation/resignation. No per-student sunset date published.
  - *Gotchas:* Availability is entirely school-dependent and varies widely - no self-serve redemption steps are published; institutional pricing is phone-only (US 888-649-2990). Students at schools without a deal must pay the discounted plan themselves.
  - *Source:* https://www.adobe.com/education.html
- **Startup program: none found** (high) — Adobe has no startup program, startup credits, or startup discount as of the verified pages: adobe.com/startups.html and adobe.com/small-business.html are 404, and adobe.com/business.html explicitly has no 'Startups' category - only Business, Students & Teachers, Home & Personal, Government, Nonprofits. A startup's only Adobe offers are the standard 7-day CC Pro trial, team plans (10-or-fewer-license 'small business' tier; e.g., Acrobat Pro teams US$28.98/mo annual-billed-monthly with free trial), and 30-day Adobe Stock trials.
  - *Eligibility:* No startup-specific eligibility (no company-age or funding cap exists because there is no program). General Adobe terms apply; team plans need a minimum of 1-2 licenses and a paying account.
  - *How to claim:* Nothing to claim - use the standard 7-day trial (one per person) and team plans; nonprofit/government categories exist but startup does not.
  - *Expiry:* N/A (no program). Team-plan trials are 14 days with a cancellation fee of half the remaining annual commitment if cancelled 14 days after the trial.
  - *Gotchas:* Don't assume a 'Adobe for Startups' exists from third-party blogs; the official pages show no such front. Cancellation fee clause applies to team plans.
  - *Source:* https://www.adobe.com/business.html

### Anthropic

- **Claude Startups program (free Claude Team year + $1,000 API credit, Oct 2026 expansion)** (high) — Launched May 2026 (originally invite-only via partner VCs; self-serve form added 21–31 May 2026). On Oct 6, 2026 Anthropic expanded it: (a) one free year of Claude Team with up to 5 Premium seats for companies new to Team (~$6,000 at the $100/seat/mo list price), (b) a one-time $1,000 API credit 'available as soon as you're approved', (c) 'Claude Startup Stack' partner discounts/credits worth up to $45,000 (combined partner max, not a cash grant), (d) up to $100,000 in additional API credits claimable through partner VCs, (e) 45-min Applied AI office hours biweekly, Claude Marketplace listing help, Founder House/events. Combined Claude value 'up to $7,000'. After a surge of '21x the expected applications' (hundreds of thousands), the free Team + $1,000 credit offers were paused ~Oct 9–13 and all applications are being re-reviewed; as of today the official page says 'Our current cohort is now full, and we're not accepting new applications right now.' Existing claimed offers are unaffected. Credits apply only to the first-party Claude API via Claude Console — not AWS Bedrock, Vertex, Azure or other third-party platforms.
  - *Eligibility:* Startups 'founded in the last five years or funded in the last two' (Oct 2026 expansion wording). Bootstrapped, pre-seed and VC-backed companies may apply. Older FAQ text for the credits tier additionally required institutional equity funding, founding within 4 years, and no prior Anthropic startup credits. Requires a Claude Console account and a company email matching the company's website domain. Geographic exclusions in the official program terms (last updated May 22, 2025): Belarus, China, Cuba, Iran, Myanmar, North Korea, Russia, Sudan, Syria, Crimea, DNR/LNR; Anthropic-affiliated persons excluded. No .edu email; no CC requirement stated.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Sign in to Claude Console; 2) submit the application at claude.com/programs/startups (currently closed — cohort full); 3) if approved, $1,000 credit and Team year activate automatically; 4) if funded, mention/partner with a partner VC for up to $100K additional credits. To hear when the next cohort opens, subscribe to the Claude Startup newsletter.
  - *Expiry:* Cohort currently full / paused as of 2026-10-22; next cohort TBA. Secondary coverage reports the $1,000 API credit expires six months after it is granted; Anthropic's general Credit Terms (cited by a Sept-2026-checked tracker) say promotional credits otherwise expire one calendar year from issuance. No published sunset for the program itself.
  - *Gotchas:* Trial-vs-free: the 'free year' is a subsidized paid-plan grant, not a permanent free tier — after it lapses the account bills at list price. The $45K figure is max combined partner value, not a grant. Credits are first-party-API only (Bedrock/Vertex not eligible). The free offers were paused within days of launch due to capacity; applying now = waitlist. VC-linked $100K tier is separate from the $1,000 and goes through the investor, not a direct Anthropic award. One award per company.
  - *Source:* https://claude.com/programs/startups
- **Claude consumer Free plan (claude.ai) — free tier, no card** (high) — The Free plan is $0/month: chat on web/desktop/mobile, web search, file creation, code execution, memory, connectors, Artifacts, Design/Slides/Docs. Verified today on the official pricing page: no credit card required ('Free: $0, no card required'). Paid plans: Pro $17/mo annual ($200 up front) or $20/mo; Max from $100/mo. Team Standard $20/seat/mo (annual) / $25 (monthly); Premium $100/seat/mo (annual).
  - *Eligibility:* Anyone; consumer account, no eligibility check beyond account creation. No .edu email. No CC required for Free.
  - *How to claim:* Sign up free at claude.ai (or download desktop/mobile app). Nothing to claim — account creation is the entire step. No API keys: the Free plan provides chat-app access only, not the first-party API.
  - *Expiry:* Ongoing; no sunset date published.
  - *Gotchas:* Not an API offer — students/startups building products cannot use the Free plan as API compute; usage limits apply (rolling 5-hour session windows, weekly caps on paid tiers).
  - *Source:* https://claude.com/pricing
- **Small free API trial credits for new users (official FAQ)** (high) — The official API pricing docs FAQ (read today): 'New users receive a small amount of free credits to test the API. Contact sales for information about extended trials for enterprise evaluation.' Also: 'Academic and research discounts might be available' (under Volume discounts). The dollar amount of the signup credit is not published on the page.
  - *Eligibility:* Any new API account (platform.claude.com console). Extended trials via sales are for enterprise evaluation; academic/research discounts are on a may-be-available basis.
  - *How to claim:* Create a Claude Console account at platform.claude.com; the small free test credit attaches to the account automatically (amount unpublished). For extended trials, email sales@anthropic.com.
  - *Expiry:* No stated expiry for the small trial credit; purchased/other credits expire per Credit Terms (promotional credits within one calendar year of issuance per a Sept-2026-checked secondary tracker).
  - *Gotchas:* A Sept-2026 third-party tracker claimed new API accounts get 'no free credit at all', which the official docs FAQ contradicts (or the small credit may not appear in every region/account — amount is unpublished, so treat as small and unspecified). A CC is needed to go beyond the free credit; billing is arrears-based.
  - *Source:* https://platform.claude.com/docs/en/about-claude/pricing
- **AI for Science program — up to $20,000 API credits for 6 months (researchers/education)** (high) — Official support article (read today): approved applicants get 'up to $20,000 in API credits to your account for a 6-month period.' Credits are for API use only ('do not apply to the Claude web app'). Launched May 5, 2025; particular focus on biology/life sciences. Review: 'We evaluate submissions on the first Monday of each month.' Single account per research program; credits allocated to one organization ID and distributed among collaborators.
  - *Eligibility:* Researchers attached to a research institution working on high-impact scientific projects (academic/nonprofit). Reviewed on scientific merit, impact, feasibility, plus biosecurity screening. No .edu email explicitly stated (institutional affiliation is the gate).
  - *How to claim:* Fill out the linked Google Form application on the AI for Science page (from the official support article at support.claude.com/en/articles/11199177 or the launch page at anthropic.com/news/ai-for-science-program). If you need models sooner, create a Console account in the interim.
  - *Expiry:* Credits valid for 6 months from grant; monthly review cycle; no program sunset date published. No nonpublic/experimental models through this program.
  - *Gotchas:* No Usage Policy exemption (red-teaming/jailbreaking research still subject to standard enforcement); rejections get no individual response; 'substantial number of applications' — competition is high; no finetuning API access via the program (finetuning preview is AWS Bedrock-only).
  - *Source:* https://support.claude.com/en/articles/11199177
- **Claude Team plan for Scientists — free Standard seats for university/nonprofit research groups** (high) — Official page (read today): Principal Investigators (or equivalents) at accredited universities and nonprofit research institutes in natural sciences, math, CS, engineering and related fields get the Team plan at promotional pricing: Standard seat $0/month (regularly $20), Premium seat $15/month (regularly $100, 5x usage), 1–25 seats per group (contact us for larger), min 1 seat. '10,000 scientists around the world can now get Claude at no cost to start.' Includes Claude Science, Claude Code & Cowork access, SSO, shared projects, database connectors. 'Claude does not train on your conversations, uploaded files, or research data.'
  - *Eligibility:* PI/equivalent at an accredited university or nonprofit research institute; for-profit, CRO and industry R&D teams are steered to paid plans. No .edu email required — institutional type + attestation (claude.ai/labs-verification/attestation: confirm affiliation + brief research description). Reviews take 5–7 business days.
  - *How to claim:* On claude.com/programs/team-plan-for-scientists: click 'Verify now' → complete the attestation → approval email → add seats immediately. For extra API credits, apply to the AI for Science grant program (separate application).
  - *Expiry:* Promotional pricing applies 12 months from sign-up, then seats renew at the then-current price for this plan. Inactivity clause: 'If a group has no activity for 90 days, we may return it to standard Team pricing' (admins notified first). Limited availability — pricing not guaranteed.
  - *Gotchas:* This is a discounted plan, not free credits — after 12 months you pay list price if you continue. No bundled API credit amount: extra usage is grant-based via AI for Science. 'Final pricing confirmed after verification' — expect surprises on larger groups.
  - *Source:* https://claude.com/programs/team-plan-for-scientists
- **Claude Campus program — student ambassador tracks with USD 3,600 stipend** (high) — Official page (read today): three student tracks, each with a flat 'USD3,600 cash stipend' (subject to program terms) and access to Anthropic-run workshops: Undergrads → Claude Builder Club (run a campus AI builder club, guest speakers, hackathons; resources shared with members include 'API credits', amount not specified); Grad students → Claude Campus Conversations (AI-and-society discussion groups, office visits); PhD/Postdoc → Claude Science workshops (researcher community, 'Claude for Science workshops'). 'Applications open September 1 and close September 12,' rolling review; program runs 'from September 2026 to June 2027' as a full school-year program. 'No. It's free to apply and free to participate.' Current status: 'The program is closed for Fall 2026.'
  - *Eligibility:* Students anywhere in the world; 18+ with work authorization in the country of study; 'AI fluency and Claude proficiency' expected but no expert background; grad/PhD tracks 'may select from a smaller pool of schools' during the pilot. No .edu email or student ID explicitly stated. No expert AI background required.
  - *How to claim:* Apply during the stated window (Sep 1–12 of the program year) via the page form at claude.com/programs/campus; rolling review; returning ambassadors must reapply and flag prior status. The current Fall 2026 round is closed — watch for the next cycle's announcement.
  - *Expiry:* Program year runs Sep 2026–Jun 2027; closed now (Fall 2026 round ended Sep 12, 2026); no automatic renewal — past ambassadors reapply each cycle. Next cycle date not published as of today.
  - *Gotchas:* The stipend is a cash payment for ambassador work (club/event running), not a research grant. 'API credits' shared with undergrad club members have no published dollar amount. 'Work authorization in your country of study' may limit international students without local work rights.
  - *Source:* https://claude.com/programs/campus
- **Claude for Teachers — free premium features for US K-12 educators** (high) — Official page (read today): 'US K-12 educators and districts get free access to premium Claude features, like teaching skills and a connection to evidence-based curricula.' Includes curriculum connectors (e.g., Learning Commons), teaching-workflow skills; 'Models are never trained on your conversations.' Districts get centrally managed accounts with RBAC and SSO plus K-12 terms and a student data privacy agreement. 'Sign up by June 30, 2027 for your full year of free access.' 'Extra usage limits apply.'
  - *Eligibility:* US K-12 educators and school districts only (region-limited to the US). Individual teachers verify identity at claude.ai/teachers; districts enroll 'every educator on your school or district domain.' No .edu email requirement per se (verification-based).
  - *How to claim:* Teachers: click 'Verify now' on claude.com/solutions/teachers → identity verification at claude.ai/teachers. Districts: district-domain enrollment (K-12 terms/privacy agreement).
  - *Expiry:* Sign up by June 30, 2027 for a full year of free access; price/plans subject to change at Anthropic's discretion. Free access duration per sign-up is one school year (rolling annual, per 'full year of free access').
  - *Gotchas:* No API access — it's a consumer/education product offering (web/desktop chat), not an API/developer free tier. US-only. 'Extra usage limits apply' — premium features still have caps.
  - *Source:* https://claude.com/solutions/teachers
- **Claude for Open Source — 6 months of free Claude Max 20x for maintainers** (high) — Official page (read today): '6 months of free Claude Max 20x' — a subscription for the Claude apps and Claude Code, not an API credit. Overage beyond Max limits billed at standard rates; when it ends 'your account returns to the free plan' (or resumes prior paid plan). Six qualifying thresholds (any one): (1) 500+ dependent repos or 100+ dependent packages, or 200,000+ combined monthly registry downloads; (2) committer/maintainer of a recognized foundation or language project (CPython, Rust team, Node.js TSC, Apache PMC, CNCF, Kubernetes, Linux kernel, Django, Rails, or similar); (3) 100+ merged PRs into repos you don't own in the last 12 months; (4) one of your repos with 20+ unique external contributors (merged PRs, last 12 months); (5) OpenSSF criticality score >= 0.4 on a repo you maintain; (6) discretionary 'ecosystem impact' catch-all. Baseline requirements (secondary, Sept-2026-checked): GitHub account 2+ years old, public activity in prior 90 days, OSI-approved license, 18+. A Sept-2026-checked tracker adds: cap of 10,000 approved recipients and a 90-day activation window on the approval link.
  - *Eligibility:* Open-source maintainers/contributors meeting one threshold; not limited to students or startups — relevant to students who maintain OSS projects. Criteria were overhauled in July 2026 (the older '5,000+ stars / 1M+ npm downloads' gate was replaced by these six thresholds).
  - *How to claim:* Apply now at claude.com/contact-sales/claude-for-oss ('Apply now' CTA; form mechanics beyond that not detailed on the page). Approved maintainers get an activation link (use within 90 days per secondary source).
  - *Expiry:* Subscription lasts 6 months; 'complimentary subscription ends' with a reminder email beforehand; account falls back to Free plan after. No program sunset date published.
  - *Gotchas:* Subscription, not API credit — does not fund API-based products. Overage after Max limits costs standard Max rates. A separate linked Terms document (anthropic.com/claude-for-oss-terms) governs details not shown on the page.
  - *Source:* https://claude.com/contact-sales/claude-for-oss
- **API code execution free hours (platform feature, de-facto free tier)** (high) — Official API pricing docs (read today): the code execution tool — used without web search/web fetch — gives 'Each organization receives 1,550 free hours of usage per month' (equivalent to the pricing page's '50 free hours of usage daily per organization'); beyond that it's $0.05/hour per container, 5-minute minimum. Also: 'Code execution is free when used with web search or web fetch' (no extra charge beyond token costs).
  - *Eligibility:* Any organization/account using the first-party API (console account); no CC-only gate mentioned for the feature, but API billing overall is arrears-based and a payment method is needed for charged usage.
  - *How to claim:* Use the code execution tool in API requests; the free 1,550 h/month allotment applies per organization automatically.
  - *Expiry:* Ongoing monthly allowance; no sunset published.
  - *Gotchas:* Token usage (input/output) is still billed normally — the free part is container runtime only. If files are included in the request, execution time is billed even if the tool isn't called (files preloaded onto the container).
  - *Source:* https://platform.claude.com/docs/en/about-claude/pricing
- **Claude via AWS Bedrock using AWS Activate credits (startup free-compute route)** (medium) — Anthropic's own program excludes Bedrock/Vertex for its credits, but startups can reach Claude through AWS Activate: founders tier $1,000–$5,000 self-funded (pre-Series B, founded within last 10 years; AWS says 'you'll hear back within 5–10 business days'); portfolio/VC-accelerator tier up to $200,000 via a VC or accelerator with an AWS Org ID; invite-only 'AWS Credits for AI Startups' tier $200,000+. The AWS Activate FAQ (checked Sept 2026) states credits 'are also redeemable for third-party models on Amazon Bedrock... like AI21 Labs, Anthropic, Cohere.' Counterpoint: Google for Startups credits are NOT usable for third-party models on Vertex, and Microsoft Foundry docs list credit-based accounts as unable to use Claude — so AWS is the viable credit path to Claude on a hyperscaler.
  - *Eligibility:* Startups pre-Series B, founded within the last 10 years (AWS Activate); US global eligibility per AWS program terms; region limits apply to AWS accounts. Not a student program (student routes on AWS are separate, e.g., AWS Educate — not verified today).
  - *How to claim:* 1) Apply for AWS Activate at aws.amazon.com/activate (self-serve or via your VC/accelerator); 2) claim the Org-ID-based credits; 3) enable Amazon Bedrock in your AWS account and select Anthropic Claude models; 4) spend Activate credits on those model calls.
  - *Expiry:* Activate credit expiry is per-AWS-timer rules (typically fixed windows per tier, not re-verified today). No Anthropic-side expiry on Bedrock usage; AWS Bedrock usage pricing differs from first-party API pricing.
  - *Gotchas:* Secondary source (checked 2026-09-25) — re-verify amounts on aws.amazon.com/activate before relying on them. Bedrock Claude pricing, rates and model availability differ from the first-party API; egress/data-transfer and Bedrock service fees can erode the credit value (data/egress costs gotcha). Anthropic startup credits cannot be used on Bedrock/Vertex — the two credit pools don't mix.
  - *Source:* https://klymentiev.com/blog/claude-free-credits
- **External Researcher Access program — $1,000 API credits for AI-safety researchers** (low) ⚠ low confidence: verify before claiming — A Sept-2026-checked tracker lists 'External Researcher Access: $1,000 in API credits' for AI safety researchers, reviewed monthly, with 'more for rare special cases.' The official page could not be located today (candidate URLs 404'd; the claude.com programs footer lists only Campus, Frontier Academy, Scientists, and Startups), so treat the dollar figure as unverified against a primary source.
  - *Eligibility:* External AI-safety researchers (individuals/researchers, distinct from the institution-based AI for Science program); exact criteria not published on a locatable official page as of today.
  - *How to claim:* Per the secondary tracker: submit a short application/request for external researcher access (monthly review); if approved, $1,000 in API credits is added to the account. Locate the canonical form via Anthropic support or the AI-safety research channel.
  - *Expiry:* Monthly review cycle; no published sunset.
  - *Gotchas:* Secondary-source only — the amount and even the program name may have changed by now; do not quote it as official without re-verifying.
  - *Source:* https://klymentiev.com/blog/claude-free-credits
- **Menlo Anthology Fund — investment + Anthropic credits for pre-seed/Series A startups** (medium) — A $100M fund run by Menlo Ventures with Anthropic: investment 'starting at $100,000' at pre-seed through Series A, bundled with Anthropic API credits. The fund's own page was internally inconsistent when checked (Aug 2026): body text says '$25,000 in free credits' while its FAQ says '$30,000 in free Anthropic credits.' Applications via an Airtable form on a rolling basis; Menlo promised responses within two weeks.
  - *Eligibility:* Pre-seed to Series A startups picked by Menlo; equity is taken (it is an investment, not a grant).
  - *How to claim:* Apply via the Airtable form at menlovc.com/menlo-anthology-fund/; if selected, the investment closes with bundled Anthropic credits. Alternatively, startups already backed by Anthropic's partner VCs can claim up to $100,000 in additional API credits 'through your VC' (stated on the official Claude Startups page read today).
  - *Expiry:* No published sunset; application form rolling; page last updated Aug 2026.
  - *Gotchas:* Equity dilution cost — this is a venture investment, not a free grant. Credit amount conflict ($25K vs $30K) on the fund page; confirm at close. The VC-claim path for up to $100K (official Claude Startups page) is the better-verified number.
  - *Source:* https://menlovc.com/menlo-anthology-fund/
- **Claude for Startups program (credits + VC top-up)** (high) — Program members access 'discounts and credits worth up to $45,000 from companies building with Claude' (offers from AI-native vendors across sales, design, data, meetings, engineering; each redeemed with a code in the Claude Console). Founders backed by a VC in Anthropic's partner network may receive up to $100K in ADDITIONAL first-party API credits, claimed through their VC. Members also get biweekly Applied AI webinars/AMAs and invites to Claude Startup events/hackathons.
  - *Eligibility:* Early-stage founders. Page states no explicit company-age cap, funding ceiling, minimum team size, .edu requirement, or region limit. The $100K is gated to founders whose VC is in the Anthropic partner network. CURRENT COHORT IS CLOSED: 'our current cohort is now full, and we're not accepting new applications right now' as of today.
  - *How to claim:* Non-members: subscribe to the Claude Startup newsletter to be alerted when the next cohort opens. Existing members: sign in via Claude Console at platform.claude.com/claude-for-startups. The $100K VC credit is claimed through the VC, not the general application.
  - *Expiry:* No published expiry/validity window for member credits. Benefits run on a membership/cohort basis. Upcoming dated event: Claude Founder House, San Francisco, Oct 6-8, 2026.
  - *Gotchas:* Credits apply ONLY to the first-party Claude API via Claude Console and CANNOT be used on AWS, Google Cloud, Azure, or other third-party platforms. The program is cohort-based and applications are currently closed (must wait for the next opening). Trial-style, membership-gated, not a self-serve signup.
  - *Source:* https://claude.com/programs/startups
- **Claude.ai Free tier (consumer, no startup needed)** (high) — Free Claude account: $0, 'Free for everyone'. Includes chat on web/desktop/mobile, web search, file creation, code execution, memory, Artifacts, Design, Slides, Docs. Projects capped at up to 5. Available models on Free: Sonnet and Haiku only (no Opus).
  - *Eligibility:* Everyone (no account prerequisites). No .edu required, no startup status required. No credit card required (listed 'n/a').
  - *How to claim:* Sign up at claude.ai; select the Free plan.
  - *Expiry:* Ongoing; not a time-limited trial. Standard per-model usage limits apply (no fixed message count published; Anthropic links to a usage-limit-best-practices article).
  - *Gotchas:* No Opus access on Free. Distinct from the paid Pro ($17-20/mo) and Max ($100+/mo) plans which DO require a credit card. Consumer chat product, not API access.
  - *Source:* https://claude.com/pricing
- **API free / trial credits** (medium) — There is NO free API tier and NO standalone free API trial credits listed on the official pricing page today. API access is pay-as-you-go (e.g., Sonnet input $2/MTok, output $10/MTok). The $45K startup credits and the 'Team plan for scientists' are the free-ish routes to API usage.
  - *Eligibility:* API requires a paid account / credit card. Free API use is only via the startup program credits or the scientist plan.
  - *How to claim:* Create an account in Claude Console (platform.claude.com) with a payment method; to use API without paying, qualify for the Claude for Startups program or the Team plan for scientists.
  - *Expiry:* n/a (no free tier to expire).
  - *Gotchas:* New API accounts need a card and are billed usage (data/egress/token costs apply). Anthropic historically offered a small free API trial credit for new accounts; that is NOT listed on the official pricing page as of 2026-10-22, so do not assume a fresh sign-up gives free API credit.
  - *Source:* https://claude.com/pricing
- **Claude Team plan for scientists (free academic/nonprofit research seats)** (high) — Standard Team seats at $0/month (list $20; annual $0/$180 per seat); Premium seats $15/month (list $100). '10,000 scientists around the world can now get Claude at no cost to start.' 1-25 seats per group (contact for larger); Premium adds 5x usage; extra usage via the AI for Science grant program.
  - *Eligibility:* Principal investigators (or equivalent) at ACCREDITED UNIVERSITIES and NONPROFIT research institutes in natural sciences, math, CS, engineering and related fields. For-profit companies, CROs, and industry R&D teams are EXCLUDED.
  - *How to claim:* Verify institutional affiliation at the 'Verify now' attestation link; confirm affiliation + brief research description; most applications reviewed within 5-7 business days; on approval admins add seats.
  - *Expiry:* Promotional $0 pricing lasts 12 months from sign-up, then seats renew at the then-current plan price; inactivity for 90 days may return the group to standard Team pricing; 'promotional pricing is not guaranteed, given limited availability.' No calendar dates published.
  - *Gotchas:* Requires an accredited-university / nonprofit-researcher affiliation (institutional, not just any .edu personal email). Cap on 10,000 scientists and limited availability. Renewal ends the free window after 12 months.
  - *Source:* https://claude.com/programs/team-plan-for-scientists
- **Claude for Teachers (free K-12 educator access)** (high) — US K-12 educators and districts get FREE access to premium Claude features (teaching skills, evidence-based-curriculum connectors aligned to state standards, e.g., Learning Commons). Individual teacher account: free, premium features, extra usage limits apply, no numeric cap published. District tier: centrally managed accounts with RBAC/SSO, K-12 privacy agreement.
  - *Eligibility:* US K-12 educators and school/district leaders. Verified via claude.ai/teachers (individual) or claude.ai/k12districts (districts). US-focused.
  - *How to claim:* Teachers: 'Verify now' -> claude.ai/teachers. Schools/districts: 'Get started' -> claude.ai/k12districts.
  - *Expiry:* Sign up by June 30, 2027 for a full year of free access; terms/price subject to change at Anthropic's discretion afterward.
  - *Gotchas:* US-only K-12. 'Extra usage limits apply' (no published numbers). Free annual window is tied to the June 30, 2027 sign-up deadline. Privacy: model training off by default for verified educators; FERPA/AFT Gold Standard oriented.
  - *Source:* https://claude.com/solutions/teachers
- **Education / university license (institutional, not free self-serve)** (medium) — A 'comprehensive university-wide plan' covering students, faculty, and staff at discounted (institutional) rates, with dedicated API credits and educational features for student learning, plus training/enablement. No specific dollar discount/seat count/percentage published; it routes to 'Contact our Education team' (/contact-sales/education-plan). Named partners include Dartmouth, UVA, U Pittsburgh, Northeastern, Syracuse, LSE, Champlain, Northumbria, USF.
  - *Eligibility:* Institutions purchasing an education plan (the .edu/affiliation benefit is institutional, not an individual free claim flow). No self-serve .edu sign-up or free student tier is offered on these pages.
  - *How to claim:* Institution contacts the Anthropic Education team via /contact-sales/education-plan; there is no individual student free-claim flow.
  - *Expiry:* No published expiry. Ongoing commercial offer.
  - *Gotchas:* NOT a free self-serve license — it is a paid institutional plan (discounted), so it is not a free offer in the same sense as the Free tier or the scientist/teacher plans. No student-per-seat free access is published.
  - *Source:* https://claude.com/solutions/education
- **2025–26 change:** As of 2026-10-22, confirmed today on official pages: (1) Claude Startups was EXPANDED on Oct 6, 2026 (free year of Claude Team up to 5 Premium seats + $1,000 one-time API credit + $45K Startup Stack + up to $100K via partner VCs), but within days (~Oct 9–13) Anthropic PAUSED the free Team/credit offers after ~21x expected applications ('currently over capacity'; all applications re-reviewed), and the official page now (Oct 22) says 'Our current cohort is now full, and we're not accepting new applications right now' — the program continues for existing members; the benefit line items were dropped from the live page. Next cohort is TBA (newsletter signup is the only official channel). (2) The original Claude Startups invite-only (partner-VC-link) model of early 2026 was replaced by a direct self-serve form between May 21–31, 2026; older guides describing the VC-link flow are outdated. (3) Claude for Open Source criteria were overhauled July 2026: the 5,000+ stars / 1M+ npm-downloads gate no longer applies, replaced by the six usage/maintainer thresholds now on the official page. (4) Claude Campus is closed for Fall 2026 (application window Sep 1–12, 2026; program runs Sep 2026–Jun 2027; next cycle not yet announced). (5) Claude for Teachers: sign-up deadline June 30, 2027 for the free-year. (6) Nothing was sunset for the consumer Free plan or the API docs' 'small free credits to test the API.' General credit terms (secondary): promotional credits expire one calendar year from issuance; the Oct 2026 startup $1,000 credit was reported to expire six months after grant — the two sources conflict, re-verify per-award. Also note: Anthropic no longer maintains a public 'startup program official terms' page beyond the May 22, 2025 version; the active program now lives under claude.com/programs/* and the domain rebranded anthropic.com → claude.com (301 redirects), so some archived anthropic.com URLs now 404 or redirect. Research caveat: the session's web-search budget was exhausted mid-run; all primary claims above were verified by direct fetch of official pages today (claude.com/pricing, claude.com/programs/startups, /campus, /team-plan-for-scientists, /frontier-academy, /contact-sales/claude-for-oss, claude.com/solutions/education and /teachers, support.claude.com article 11199177, anthropic.com/news/ai-for-science-program, platform.claude.com/docs pricing); items marked medium/low come from the klymentiev.com tracker (checked 2026-09-25) and Oct 2026 press (TechCrunch, CNBC, 9to5AI, Times of India) or the fund's own page (Aug 2026).
- **2025–26 change:** As of 2026-10-22 the Claude for Startups program has changed materially from its 2024-2025 form: it is now run in cohorts, and the current cohort is FULL with no new applications accepted, so the $45K/$100K credits are only claimable when the next cohort opens (newsletter sign-up is the only current action). No program is sunset, but two notes: (1) The Claude API no longer lists a standalone free trial-credit offer for new accounts on the official pricing page — free API usage is only via the startup credits or the Team plan for scientists, so any historical small free API trial should not be assumed. (2) Two genuinely free education-side offers DO exist today and are the effective 'education' equivalents of a free tier: the Claude Team plan for scientists (standard seats $0 for 12 months for accredited-university/nonprofit PIs, then renews at paid price) and Claude for Teachers (free premium access for US K-12 educators, sign up by June 30, 2027 for the free year). A university-wide 'education license' exists but is a paid discounted institutional plan, not a free self-serve student offer. WebSearch budget was exhausted this session, so claims above were verified by reading the official pages directly today (claude.com/programs/startups, /pricing, /solutions/education, /programs/team-plan-for-scientists, /solutions/teachers); figures like the $45K/$100K amounts and the $0 scientist pricing were confirmed on the pages, but I could not cross-check third-party news coverage of 2025-2026 changes.
- **Critic correction** (Anthropic Claude API): Caveat needed (secondary source). No general free API tier was found. Claude for Startups, expanded Oct 6, 2026, now includes a free year of Claude Team for qualifying startups and a one-time $1,000 API credit. Anthropic says it is over capacity on those offers and is re-reviewing applications. Confirm on anthropic.com.
- **Critic correction** (Claude for Startups (Anthropic)): Figures are misleading (secondary source). The up-to-$45K figure is partner-offer value in the Claude Startup Stack, not API credit. The direct API credit is a one-time $1,000. VC-backed startups in Anthropic's partner network may get up to $100K in API credits, claimed through their VC, and those credits cannot be used on Amazon Bedrock.
- **Critic correction** (No free API tier or developer credit program): Monthly API credits exist for subscribers: Max 5x gets $100/month, Max 20x gets $200/month, and Team gets $20 per Standard seat or $100 per Premium seat (pooled, capped at $500/month). Claude Startups members get a one-time $1,000 API credit, but applications were closed as of Oct 13, 2026.
- **Critic correction** (Up to $45K in Claude API credits/discounts): The $45,000 is partner discounts and credits for tools from companies building with Claude (the Claude Startup Stack), not Claude API credits. The Claude API credit is a one-time $1,000. Applications were closed as of Oct 13, 2026.

### Autodesk Education

- **Autodesk Education Plan — free 1-year professional licenses for students** (high) — Free one-year, single-user access to 75+ professional products (AutoCAD incl. LT/toolsets, Revit, Maya, 3ds Max, Fusion, Inventor, Civil 3D, Alias, Arnold, Flow Studio, Tinkercad, etc.) with the SAME features/functionality as paid subscriptions (not a reduced trial). Access includes current version plus up to 3 prior versions where available; English (some titles in more languages). Install on up to 3 devices. No credit card required — eligibility is verified via SheerID (proof of enrollment). Cloud SaaS services (rendering, simulation, Autodesk Drive storage) are included but users who regularly consume beyond 'market average' may face restrictions that can change without notice. Autodesk Flow Studio requires post-secondary students age 18+.
  - *Eligibility:* Students enrolled at a qualified (government-accredited) educational institution — middle/high schools, junior colleges, universities, technical schools; meets minimum age requirement. Also eligible: homeschooled students (documented membership in a nationally recognized homeschooling body), students doing thesis/master's work, apprentices (Austria, Germany, Switzerland, UK only), registered participants in Autodesk-sponsored competitions. Typically verified with a school-provided (.edu or equivalent) email + SheerID verification; 'most educational institutions worldwide qualify' except countries under US trade embargo. Graduates lose eligibility but keep the account (and can buy paid licenses); school email can be changed if not using SSO. Minimum age: required per TOU (13+, per Autodesk account terms).
  - *How to claim:* 1) Create/complete an Autodesk account at autodesk.com/education/edu-software (or get assigned a product by a teacher via institution account). 2) Confirm eligibility on the Education Products page using your school-provided email (SheerID may ask for proof of enrollment). 3) Click 'Complete Account Setup' from the confirmation email. 4) Download/install from the Autodesk Account 'All Products and Services' page; sign in to start. Alt path: educator's institution account assigns products — students skip individual verification but still need their own account.
  - *Expiry:* 1-year term; renewable annually while eligibility (enrollment) continues. Autodesk emails a reminder 30 days before expiration; missing the renewal forces a full restart of access and any subscriptions, even for already-installed products. Check expiration on the Education Products page banner.
  - *Gotchas:* NOT trial-vs-free confusion: this is the full professional product, but licensed for EDUCATIONAL PURPOSES ONLY — no commercial, professional, or for-profit work; also excluded: campus facilities design and commercially affiliated research. Cloud add-ons (rendering/simulation/storage) can be throttled for heavy users. SheerID profile details can't be changed after submission — keep them matching school records. Verification repeats every year. From Aug 7, 2026 education accounts must use SSO (school credentials) instead of standalone Autodesk passwords. Output/work may not be used commercially even with free tools. US trade-embargoed countries excluded (export compliance).
  - *Source:* https://www.autodesk.com/support/account/education/students-educators/overview
- **Autodesk Education Plan — educators and qualified institutions (free classroom/lab licenses)** (high) — Free 1-year, renewable access for educators (employees/independent contractors whose primary purpose is teaching enrolled students at a qualified institution). An educator creates an Autodesk account FOR THE INSTITUTION and can assign/unassign products to up to 250 students per product; assigned students don't re-verify (eligibility inherits from the educator) but each still needs their own account. Institutions also get web services like Fusion for labs.
  - *Eligibility:* Employees or contractors of accredited middle/high schools, junior colleges, universities, technical schools, or recognized homeschool bodies. NOT eligible: non-accredited institutions, training centers, churches, hospitals/health-care systems, public libraries, research labs, retraining programs; Autodesk Certified Instructors at ATC partners get genuine ATC licenses instead. Most countries qualify except US trade-embargoed countries.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Go to Get Products (autodesk.com/education/edu-software) and select products. 2) Confirm eligibility with school-provided email (SheerID). 3) Create the institution's Autodesk account via the 'Complete Account Setup' email link. 4) Even if software is already installed, click 'Download' on the product card to trigger the entitlement. 5) Admins can enable SSO and assign products to up to 250 students per product.
  - *Expiry:* Same as student plan: 1 year, renewable annually while employed/contracting at a qualified institution; renew before expiry or restart. Note region-specific sunset of new network/multi-seat licenses (see sunset_notes).
  - *Gotchas:* Educational use only — no facilities use (designing/maintaining the school's own buildings) and no research run by commercial affiliates. ACIs are excluded. Multi-seat/network licensing is being phased out in favor of institution subscriptions (see sunset_notes), so long-term lab plans should migrate to Education institution subscriptions.
  - *Source:* https://www.autodesk.com/support/account/education/admin/eligibility
- **30-day free trials (users who don't qualify for the Education Plan)** (medium) — Autodesk's standard 30-day product trial remains available to users who aren't eligible for the Education Plan (trial vs free: this is a time-boxed trial of the full commercial product, not the free education license). Official pages explicitly state trial participants may NOT use trials for training purposes.
  - *Eligibility:* Anyone not eligible for the Education Plan (e.g., training-center participants, unaccredited program attendees). No enrollment verification needed.
  - *How to claim:* Select 'Try' / 30-day trial on product pages at autodesk.com, create an Autodesk account. (Trial page specifics not re-verified today — site blocked this fetcher; see confidence.)
  - *Expiry:* 30 days from activation, no renewal; after trial you must buy a paid subscription.
  - *Gotchas:* No training use permitted. Historically Autodesk trials don't require a credit card, but that was NOT re-verifiable today (autodesk.com returned HTTP 403 to the fetcher and the session web-search budget was exhausted), so treat the no-CC claim as unconfirmed. Trial licenses are commercial licenses — unlike the education plan, no usage restriction to education, but full paid version is commercial-only.
  - *Source:* https://www.autodesk.com/support/account/education/students-educators/eligibility
- **Tinkercad — free consumer tier (always free, no enrollment needed)** (medium) — Tinkercad (simple 3D design, 3D printing, circuits) is listed on Autodesk's education products page and is free to the general public; unlike the 1-year education plan it does not expire for casual use and needs no .edu email. It also remains available under the Education Plan as one of the included products.
  - *Eligibility:* Anyone with an Autodesk account; no eligibility verification, no age restriction stated beyond account terms; no region restriction found.
  - *How to claim:* Sign up at tinker.com (now autodesk.com/tinker) — free, no credit card.
  - *Expiry:* No stated expiration (persistent free product, not a 1-year subscription like the rest of the education catalog).
  - *Gotchas:* Cloud-based only; data/egress and export limits apply per Autodesk's service terms. Free tier vs paid Tinkercad Pro: Pro adds advanced export/assembly features — free tier has export/length limits that were not re-verified today.
  - *Source:* https://www.autodesk.com/education/edu-software/overview
- **Autodesk startup program — no active free startup offer found** (low) ⚠ low confidence: verify before claiming — As of 2026-10-22 no free 'Autodesk for Startups' / startup-credits program appears in today's search results for autodesk.com, and the education fronts are student/.edu-only. An earlier-generation Autodesk startup program (1-year professional licenses / discounts for young startups) could NOT be re-verified today: autodesk.com returned HTTP 403 to the fetcher and the session's web-search budget (200 shared calls) was exhausted on the startup-specific query. Treat any current startup program as unconfirmed; the safe conclusion is that Autodesk currently has no verifiable free tier, trial credits, or startup grant aimed at startups — free access is via the Education Plan (students/educators) or 30-day commercial trials.
  - *Eligibility:* Unknown/unverified — no startup age/funding-cap criteria found in any official page retrieved today.
  - *How to claim:* Not determinable today. If one exists it would live under autodesk.com/company/startups or autodesk.com/education; neither could be fetched (403) or re-searched (budget exhausted).
  - *Expiry:* Unknown.
  - *Gotchas:* Do not cite a startup program without re-verification once autodesk.com is fetchable again; historical programs may have been folded into the 'Autodesk 10x' / A360-era discounts or discontinued in 2024-2025.
  - *Source:* https://www.autodesk.com/education/edu-software/overview
- **2025–26 change:** 2025–2026 changes confirmed from official pages read today: (1) Single Sign-On mandate — Autodesk Education accounts will be required to use SSO (school credentials) starting August 7, 2026; schools like SMU enabled it in Aug 2025 (blog.smu.edu, 2025-08-07). (2) Multi-seat sunset — the US Education products page states 'Starting December 31, 2027, Autodesk will no longer offer new network and multi-seat licenses' and pushes institutions to Education institution subscriptions; the Asia-Pacific page (dated May 2025) states the cutoff as March 2026 for that region — i.e., the network/multi-seat lab-license model is being rolled off region by region starting 2026, so multi-seat classroom plans should be migrated now. (3) Startup program — status unverifiable as of 2026-10-22 (403 fetch blocks + exhausted shared web-search budget); no active free startup offer appeared in results. (4) No evidence today that the core 1-year free education license for students/educators changed, was reduced, or sunset — it remains 1-year, renewable, 3-device, full-feature. (5) Consumer free tiers (e.g., Fusion personal-use, AutoCAD Web basic) were NOT re-verified today and should be treated as unconfirmed; if they were sunset, that would land here after re-verification.

### AWS (Amazon Web Services)

- **AWS Free plan (new-account credits: $100 base + up to $100 earned, 6-month window)** (high) — As of 2026 AWS replaced the classic 12-month per-service free tier with a Free plan vs Paid plan model. New accounts are auto-enrolled in the Free plan and get $100 in AWS credits immediately, plus up to $100 more earned by completing 'Explore AWS' activities (up to $200 total). The Free plan runs at no cost for up to 6 months or until credits run out, whichever comes first, and the account closes automatically. Base credits expire 6 months from account creation; activity-earned credits expire 12 months; if you upgrade to the Paid plan remaining credits apply to bills and expire 12 months after account creation. 30+ 'always free' services with monthly caps remain on both plans. A payment method is NOT required to sign up for most new customers (a major change from the old credit-card-required model). Credits are USD, non-transferable between accounts, and can offset usage on eligible services including Amazon Bedrock model invocations and AI/ML workloads. Featured Bedrock access on the Free plan; Amazon Bedrock AgentCore is listed as a Paid-plan-exclusive service.
  - *Eligibility:* Any new AWS account (personal or business). No .edu requirement, no funding criteria, no credit card required for most customers. Free plan restricts access to a subset of services; Paid plan allows a monthly spend limit starting at $20.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Create a new AWS account (auto-enrolls in Free plan, no payment method needed for most customers) -> 2) $100 credits are applied on signup -> 3) Complete Explore AWS activities in the console to earn up to $100 more -> 4) Use credits on eligible services incl. Bedrock within the 6-month window -> 5) Optionally upgrade to Paid plan; remaining credits roll onto invoices until 12-month expiry.
  - *Expiry:* Base $100 credits: 6 months from account creation. Earned up to $100: 12 months from account creation. Free plan itself auto-closes 6 months after account open or when credits are exhausted. No renewal or extension; credits cannot be transferred.
  - *Gotchas:* Trial-vs-free confusion: this is a 6-month credit window, NOT the old 12-month tier. Once upgraded to the Paid plan you CANNOT downgrade back to Free. Legacy per-service allowances (EC2 750 hrs, S3 5 GB, Bedrock free-token lists) no longer appear in the new structure. Data transfer/egress and some advanced features may be excluded or Paid-plan-only. The old Bedrock 12-month free-token offer is gone from the free-tier FAQ and docs (see sunset notes). Credits only offset eligible services - check the Free Tier Categories list.
  - *Source:* https://aws.amazon.com/free/free-tier-faqs/(alsohttps://aws.amazon.com/free/)
- **AWS Activate Founders tier (self-serve; $1,000 start, up to $5,000)** (high) — The self-serve AWS Activate tier. $1,000 in AWS Activate Credits to start; select startups may qualify for additional credits up to $5,000 over time. Credits (USD, subject to AWS Promotional Credit T&Cs) can offset 200+ eligible AWS services including third-party foundation models on Amazon Bedrock (Anthropic Claude, OpenAI, Mistral AI, Meta, etc.) via a single API. Application is reviewed within 5-10 business days.
  - *Eligibility:* Self-funded / bootstrapped (no VC or accelerator backing needed). Must be: new to AWS Activate Credits, have a fully functioning company website, founded in the past 10 years, Pre-Series B, and have an AWS account on a Paid Tier plan (note: the new paid-plan requirement means the free-plan-only account must be upgraded). No .edu requirement. Business email matching the startup domain is required for the application.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Create/verify an AWS Builder ID (personal email) -> 2) Complete the one-time AWS Activate profile with your business email -> 3) Choose the Founders tier -> 4) Provide startup details (product, funding stage, company profile) -> 5) Link an AWS account (on Paid plan) and submit -> 6) Hear back in 5-10 business days. Apply at https://aws.amazon.com/startups/credits/ ('Apply here' / 'Join now' on https://aws.amazon.com/startups/).
  - *Expiry:* Promotional credits: no public per-tier expiry stated on the credits page today; AWS has said Activate credits generally expire in 1-3 years depending on package (the $200K-class credits are on 3-year expiry per the 2024 announcement). Credits are in USD, subject to Promotional Credit T&Cs, non-transferable.
  - *Gotchas:* Only $1,000 is guaranteed; the $5,000 extension is selective. 'New to Activate Credits' - you cannot re-claim unless you are requesting more than previously received. Egress/data-transfer and some fees may be excluded from promotional credits (check the credits FAQ 'What is not covered'). Requiring a Paid-plan AWS account means a card/payment setup may be needed at the account level even though the Free plan itself needs no payment method.
  - *Source:* https://aws.amazon.com/startups/credits/
- **AWS Activate Portfolio tier (provider-backed; up to $200,000)** (high) — Up to $200,000 in AWS Activate Credits (the tier ceiling was $100,000 until the June 2024 doubling for Series A-stage startups; the credits page now lists a single Portfolio tier up to $200,000). Usable on 200+ services including third-party Bedrock models (Anthropic, OpenAI, Mistral, Meta, etc.), and can also be applied to Developer/Business AWS Support Plans. Program has provided $8B+ in credits to 350,000+ startups since 2013.
  - *Eligibility:* Pre-Series B (most recent funding round within the last 12 months, if applicable), founded in the past 10 years, fully functioning company website, AWS account on a Paid Tier plan, new to Activate Credits or requesting more than previously received, AND an Organization ID (Org ID) from an AWS Activate Provider (VC firm, accelerator, incubator or angel group in the AWS network - e.g. YC, Sequoia, a16z, Greylock). Apply within 12 months of your most recent funding date.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Confirm your VC/accelerator/angel is an AWS Activate Provider and obtain their Org ID -> 2) Create AWS Builder ID + complete Activate profile with business email -> 3) Choose Portfolio tier and enter the provider's Org ID (or use a direct provider invite) -> 4) Link AWS account and submit at https://aws.amazon.com/startups/credits/ -> 5) Reviewed in 5-10 business days; approved credits are added to your AWS account.
  - *Expiry:* The $200K credit package expires 3 years from issuance (per the June 2024 change; previously the $100K package expired in 1 year). USD, subject to Promotional Credit T&Cs, non-transferable.
  - *Gotchas:* You cannot apply yourself without a provider's Org ID or invite - a consulting partner cannot file it for you. 'Within 12 months of your latest funding date' is a hard window. Credits cannot be sold or transferred; check the 'not covered' list (data transfer and some support fees historically excluded, though Support Plan application is permitted). The old ~$100K ceiling is outdated in most third-party guides.
  - *Source:* https://aws.amazon.com/startups/credits/
- **AWS Credits for AI Startups (invite-only; $200,000+)** (high) — A new tier launched with the 2026 credits-page redesign: $200,000+ in AWS Credits for AI startups 'ready to scale' beyond the Activate tiers. No open application exists - it is invite-only and accessed by talking to your AWS Account Manager. Additional credits beyond Activate are explicitly marketed for AI startups building on Amazon Bedrock (funding models from Anthropic, OpenAI, Mistral, Meta, etc.).
  - *Eligibility:* Startups that have already gone through Activate Portfolio and are ready to scale (AI-focused). No public age/funding/.edu criteria published; eligibility is determined case-by-case by the account team. No credit-card specifics published (linked to the existing AWS account).
  - *How to claim:* No self-serve claim. 1) Be an AWS Activate participant (Portfolio tier) -> 2) Contact your AWS Account Manager (or use 'If you need additional support, contact us' on https://aws.amazon.com/startups/credits/) -> 3) AWS reviews and issues credits to your AWS account.
  - *Expiry:* Not published today; by analogy with the $200K Activate package, 3-year expiry is expected but unconfirmed. USD, promotional T&Cs apply.
  - *Gotchas:* Invite-only means no guaranteed outcome or timeline; it is not a free product - it is a promotional credit negotiation. Amounts are not fixed ('$200,000+' is a floor, not a promise).
  - *Source:* https://aws.amazon.com/startups/credits/
- **Kiro Startup Credits (up to a year of Kiro Pro+ credits; Starter/Growth/Scale tiers)** (high) — New AWS Kiro agentic-IDE credit offer (linked from the AWS credits page: 'Start building on day one... Start with Kiro Startup Credits, then grow into Activate Credits'). Up to one year's worth of Kiro Pro+ credits in three tiers by team size: Starter (up to 2 Kiro users), Growth (up to 10 users), Scale (up to 30 users). Credits expire 1 year after issuance to your AWS account; they cannot be refunded, re-issued, transferred or sold. Separately, Kiro main offers a $20 credit when you first sign up for a paid Kiro plan via social login or Builder ID.
  - *Eligibility:* Early-stage through Series A startups, global. Requires an official business email on the startup's own domain AND an AWS Account ID using a matching email. EXCLUDED REGIONS: Argentina, Belarus, Brazil, China/GCR, Cuba, France, Germany, Iran, Italy, Mexico, North Korea, Poland, Russia, Spain, Syria, Crimea, DNR, LNR, UAE. Startups with active AWS Activate Credits are NOT eligible for this promotion (though Activate credits can still be applied toward Kiro). No .edu requirement.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Sign in with your AWS account -> 2) Apply via 'Apply for startup credits' (routes to https://aws.amazon.com/startups/credits/kiro) -> 3) Rolling review; decision by email -> 4) Approved credits are automatically added to your AWS account and activate immediately (no deferral) -> 5) To use them, log into Kiro via AWS IAM Identity Center (Kiro Enterprise) - social login (GitHub/Google) and Builder ID do NOT work with startup credits. After credits run out, standard Kiro Pro+ billing applies.
  - *Expiry:* Credits expire 1 year from date of issuance to the AWS account. One application per startup at a time; re-application allowed only if eligibility status changed after a rejection. After 12 months of credit consumption you move to paid Kiro Pro+.
  - *Gotchas:* The excluded-region list is long (much of the EU/MEA/APAC). Activation cannot be deferred, so issuance date = countdown start. Company-level credit pool shared only among authorized users of associated accounts. It is mutually exclusive with holding active AWS Activate Credits.
  - *Source:* https://kiro.dev/startups/
- **Kiro Student Offer (1,000 credits/month free for 12 months)** (high) — Kiro's student program: 1,000 Kiro credits per month at no cost for one full year, explicitly 'no credit card required'. After the year, the account auto-downgrades to the public Kiro free tier (projects/specs stay on your machine and repos; no discounted paid plan for students). 1,000 is the Kiro credit unit for model usage (Kiro Pro+ is its paid tier).
  - *Eligibility:* Enrolled student at one of ~150+ listed eligible universities (US-centric: MIT, CMU, NYU, Georgia Tech, ASU, etc., plus international). Account must use a university-affiliated email (.edu, .ca or equivalent) via Google, GitHub, or AWS Builder ID. Must be at least 18 years old. If your school isn't listed you can submit your university email through a 'Don't see your school listed?' form and Kiro may reach out later.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Create or log into a Kiro account using your university email -> 2) On the account usage page, a banner appears if eligible; click verify and complete a SheerID check (first/last name, DOB, university; sometimes student ID or enrollment letter) -> 3) Once verified, the 1,000 credits/month activate automatically.
  - *Expiry:* 12 months from verification, then auto-downgrade to the public free tier. Credits do NOT roll over between monthly cycles; hitting the 1,000 cap means waiting for the next cycle; no add-on credit purchases on the student tier.
  - *Gotchas:* No rollover of unused monthly credits; the ~150-university list is the real gate (many schools not included); SheerID can delay activation; after year one there is no student discount - only the base free tier. This is Kiro (agentic IDE credits for coding with frontier models, incl. Claude-class models), NOT Amazon Bedrock API access - for direct Bedrock API use, the AWS Free plan credits or Activate credits are the path.
  - *Source:* https://kiro.dev/students/
- **AWS Academy (education license - free courses, not cloud credits)** (low) ⚠ low confidence: verify before claiming — AWS Academy is AWS's education program (successor to AWS Educate, renamed ~2021) giving students and educators free structured AWS courses, hands-on labs via partner platforms, and discount pathways toward certification. It is a training/education license: it does NOT include AWS credits, Bedrock access, or LLM free tiers - students who want actual Bedrock usage must use the AWS Free plan, Activate, or Kiro offers above. (Could not be re-verified live today: https://aws.amazon.com/academy/ returned 404 and the training sub-page was unreachable; details from general knowledge.)
  - *Eligibility:* Open to students and teachers at partner institutions; typically .edu/institutional affiliation or partnership enrollment (e.g., via AWS Academy Accelerate Your Career or institutional programs). No credit card needed for the free learning content.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Enroll through your institution's AWS Academy partnership or an open course on the AWS Academy/Skill Builder platform -> 2) Complete free courses -> 3) Certifications and advanced access are paid or partner-subsidized.
  - *Expiry:* N/A - ongoing educational access; no credit expiry because no credits are granted.
  - *Gotchas:* Not an LLM offer at all - it is courses/labs. Do not confuse with free Bedrock or Kiro student credits. Institutional access rules vary by country/partner.
  - *Source:* https://aws.amazon.com/academy/
- **AWS Free Account (new credit-based Free plan) — the restructured AWS Free Tier** (high) — New AWS accounts automatically enter a credit-based Free plan: $100 USD credits at sign-up + up to $100 more earned by activating foundational services = up to $200 total, spendable across 90+ services. Free plan runs up to 6 months (account auto-closes at 6 months or when credits run out, whichever is first). 30+ services also have recurring 'Always Free' monthly limits on both plans. On the Paid-plan path, the same credits are valid for up to 12 months from account creation. Free-plan accounts are migrated from the legacy usage-based 12-month Free Tier for accounts created on/after July 15, 2025; a new sign-up experience launched October 2026 (older accounts still use the legacy credits console view).
  - *Eligibility:* New AWS customers only (existing/previous account holders excluded). No .edu requirement; open to students and startups alike. Payment method NOT required to sign up for most new customers, but AWS may request a payment method or extra info to verify identity. Unavailable in China (Beijing and Ningxia) regions and AWS GovCloud (US) (with a note that some services such as Lambda are exceptions in GovCloud).
  - *How to claim:* 1) Create a new AWS (Builder ID) account via the registration portal — Free plan is selected automatically; 2) use the $100 signup credit, then earn up to $100 more by activating foundational services (credits appear in billing console within ~30 min); 3) optionally upgrade to Paid (remaining credits become bill credits expiring 12 months post-creation).
  - *Expiry:* Free-plan credits: 6 months from account opening or exhaustion, whichever first; account then suspends, data retained 90 days, then account and data permanently erased if not upgraded. Paid-path credits: 12 months from account creation. No renewal; downgrading Paid back to Free is not allowed.
  - *Gotchas:* Legacy 12-month per-service Free Tier no longer exists for new accounts. Activating 'advanced features' is irreversible and voids remaining earnable credits; joining an AWS Organization or deploying a Control Tower landing zone immediately voids remaining earnable credits. Paid-only services are excluded from the Free plan. After 6 months the account closes itself — you lose access to resources/data unless you upgrade within the 90-day grace window. Egress/data transfer and overages beyond limits are paid.
  - *Source:* https://aws.amazon.com/free/free-tier-faqs/(plushttps://aws.amazon.com/free/)
- **AWS Activate — Founders tier (self-funded/bootsrap startups)** (high) — $1,000 in AWS Activate credits guaranteed; select participants may qualify for up to $5,000 over time. Credits apply to 200+ AWS services (compute, RDS, DynamoDB, S3, CloudFront) and Amazon Bedrock, so founders can evaluate/customize/deploy third-party models from OpenAI, Mistral AI, Anthropic, and Meta. Excluded: Mechanical Turk, AWS Managed Services, AWS Professional Services, AWS Training & Certification. Credits apply monthly to new usage, never retroactively. Application processed within 5-10 business days.
  - *Eligibility:* Self-funded/bootsrap startup (no VC backing needed), founded within the last 10 years, pre-Series B, AWS account on the Paid plan, either new to Activate credits or requesting more than previously received. No .edu or institutional requirement; professional email required for the Builder ID.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Create an AWS Builder ID with a professional email; 2) select the Founders tier at /startups/join?destination=/credits/apply; 3) provide product, funding-stage and company-profile details; 4) link your AWS account to the Builder ID and submit; decisions in 5-10 business days.
  - *Expiry:* Credits 'usually expire within 1-2 years depending on the package' (official FAQ wording). No renewal, but you may reapply if you request a higher amount than previously approved and haven't hit the lifetime credit cap.
  - *Gotchas:* The extra $4,000 (up to $5,000) is discretionary ('select participants'), not guaranteed. Requires a Paid-plan AWS account, so expect a billing relationship even if credits cover usage. Credits don't cover AWS Professional/Managed Services or certification fees. $1,000-5,000 amounts are small vs the Portfolio tier — check provider-backed tracks first if you have accelerator/VC contact.
  - *Source:* https://aws.amazon.com/startups/credits/(tiers)andhttps://aws.amazon.com/aws-startups/learn/everything-you-need-to-know-about-aws-activate-credits/(expiry/Bedrockdetails)
- **AWS Activate — Portfolio tier (VC/accelerator/angel-funded startups)** (high) — Up to $200,000 in AWS Activate credits for VC/accelerator/angel-backed startups, claimed with an Organization ID issued by the participating provider (network includes Andreessen Horowitz, Brex, Carta, Greylock, Sequoia, Y Combinator; 'thousands' of providers). Providers structure multi-step offers (FAQ example: $25K at pre-seed stepping up to $100K at seed; you receive the difference between tiers). Credits usable on 200+ services including Amazon Bedrock third-party models (OpenAI, Mistral, Anthropic, Meta).
  - *Eligibility:* Backed by an Activate Provider (VC, accelerator, angel investor, or enabling organization); founded within the last 10 years; pre-Series B (the /free/offers/ hub phrases it as 'up to and including Pre-Seed, Angel, Seed, Debt Finance, and Series A'); must apply within 12 months of the most recent funding date; affiliation with the provider must still be active; AWS account on Paid plan; new to Activate or requesting more than previously received.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Get the provider's Organization ID from your VC/accelerator; 2) apply at /startups/join?destination=/credits/apply selecting Portfolio tier and entering the Org ID; 3) submit company/funding details and link your AWS account; 4) response in 5-10 business days (offers hub says 7-10). Alternatively the provider portal can claim on your behalf.
  - *Expiry:* Per package, credits usually expire within 1-2 years (official FAQ). Reapply for a higher tier amount if you've raised further, as long as you're under the lifetime cap and still affiliated; you receive the difference between the old and new tier.
  - *Gotchas:* Without an active provider relationship you cannot access this tier (fall back to Founders). The /free/offers/ hub still shows the Activate range as '$1,000 to $100,000', contradicting the credits page/FAQ's $200,000 portfolio ceiling — the offers hub appears stale. Credits don't cover excluded services (Professional/Managed Services, Training & Certification) and never retroactively apply to past bills. Applying outside the 12-month post-funding window is not allowed.
  - *Source:* https://aws.amazon.com/startups/credits/andhttps://aws.amazon.com/aws-startups/learn/everything-you-need-to-know-about-aws-activate-credits/
- **AWS Credits for AI Startups (invite-only, above the $200K Portfolio cap)** (high) — $200,000+ in additional AWS credits, positioned for 'startups ready to scale post Activate Portfolio' — i.e., AI startups that have exhausted the Portfolio ceiling. Official FAQ wording: 'Additional AWS Credits above $200,000 are available for AI startups ready to scale beyond Activate's tiers.' Invite only; no public dollar figure beyond '200,000+'.
  - *Eligibility:* Invite-only; AI-focused startups already at/above the $200,000 Activate Portfolio ceiling (or approaching it). AWS reaches out; existing customers work through their Account Manager. No public self-serve application.
  - *How to claim:* No public claim path. Routes: (a) AWS outreach/invitation; (b) ask your AWS Account Manager or Activate provider about additional AI-startup credits; (c) the /startups/credits/ page routes AI-tier interest to the account-manager channel.
  - *Expiry:* Governed by the AWS Promotional Credit T&C; per the Activate FAQ, Activate credits generally burn down over 1-2 years depending on package. Exact expiry for the AI tier is not published.
  - *Gotchas:* Amount, eligibility thresholds and even the program's public name are not itemized on the credits page — treat specifics as account-manager-mediated. Do not confuse with Kiro credits or the Founders/Portfolio tiers. Credits still exclude AWS Professional/Managed Services.
  - *Source:* https://aws.amazon.com/startups/credits/('AWSCreditsforAIStartups'card)andFAQhttps://aws.amazon.com/aws-startups/learn/everything-you-need-to-know-about-aws-activate-credits/
- **Kiro startup credits (1 year of Kiro Pro+ IDE access)** (high) — Up to 12 months of Kiro Pro+ credits (AWS's agentic AI IDE) in three team tiers: Starter (up to 2 Kiro users), Growth (up to 10 users), Scale (up to 30 users). No USD figure published. Issued at the company level, so authorized users on associated AWS accounts can share it. Once exhausted, the account automatically switches to standard Kiro Pro+ billing.
  - *Eligibility:* Early-stage to Series A startups; a business email matching your company domain AND a matching AWS account ID. Startups with ACTIVE AWS Activate credits are NOT eligible (though existing Activate credits can be spent on Kiro).
