Note: session web-search budget was exhausted, so 2025-2026 change verification relied on WebFetch of official AWS pages (free/, free/offers/, free/free-tier-faqs/, free/terms/, startups/, education/, EC2 docs, Lambda pricing, RDS free page, what's-new Jul 2025 entry, AWS blog 15 Jul 2025) rather than broader search.
- **2025–26 change:** Material 2025-2026 changes (all verified against AWS pages today, 2026-10-22): (1) Jul 15, 2025 — the legacy "12 months free" tier was replaced for NEW accounts by a credit-based Free Tier: $100 sign-up credit + up to $100 earned, 6-month Free plan; every legacy 12-month offer on pre-Jul-15-2025 accounts (including legacy CloudWatch 12-month items) has now fully lapsed. (2) Oct 2026 — AWS launched a new sign-up experience: social sign-in (Google/GitHub/Apple/Amazon), no credit card needed for most customers, and credit tracking moved from the Billing console to a Settings Portal for accounts signed up after the rollout; the Free Tier FAQ now states base credits expire 6 months (vs. 12 months in the Jul 9, 2025 Terms) — a live contradiction AWS hasn't harmonized, so treat 6 months as the safe planning horizon. (3) Dec 3, 2025 — Kiro Startup Credits v1 launched (up to 100 users, pre-seed to Series B, apply by Dec 31, 2025) and closed; relaunched Apr 7, 2026 with 2/10/30-user tiers, early-stage to Series A (VC-backed per the current promotion), apply by Dec 31, 2026; startups with active AWS Activate credits are excluded from the Kiro promo. (4) Jun 2024 (context) — AWS doubled Portfolio credits $100K to $200K and extended expiry 1 year to 3 years; still the structure in effect. (5) Feb 2023 (context) — AWS Educate stopped issuing universal/institutional credit grants; it is now free learning only, and student credits now flow through Builder Center Student Rewards (up to $579 incl. $30 credits + $100 exam voucher, launched Aug 2026). (6) CloudFront's classic "100 GB/month always-free" line is now presented as a standing $0 "Free" plan tier with 100 GB + 1M requests + 5 GB S3 storage credits and no overage charges — not sunset, but restructured. No CloudWatch/observability offer was sunset; the known limits (10 metrics, 1M API requests, 5 GB logs, 100 canaries, 3 dashboards, 10 alarms, 100K X-Ray traces) all still apply as ALWAYS-FREE allowances. There is no CloudWatch-specific startup or student grant beyond the general AWS Free Tier credits and Activate credits, which can offset CloudWatch usage on a Paid plan.
- **2025–26 change:** Major 2025-2026 changes verified on official pages today (2026-10-22): (1) The legacy 12-month Free Tier is gone — the old 'free for 12 months' language no longer appears on aws.amazon.com/free; it has been replaced by the credit-based Free Plan ($100 signup + up to $100 explore = up to $200 over 6 months), after which the account auto-closes (data kept 90 days, then erased). (2) The classic 'Always Free' list was narrowed in messaging to '30+ services' with monthly limits (legacy tier had 60+ services); per-service quota numbers are no longer published on the hub. (3) SageMaker notebook free allowance shrunk to 250 hours of sc.t3.medium for the first 2 months (vs legacy 750 hours/month ml.t3.medium for 12 months). (4) AWS Activate tiers renamed/revalued: 'Activate Community' no longer appears on the current credits page — it is now 'Activate Founders' (up to $5,000, start $1,000) alongside 'Activate Portfolio' (now up to $200,000, previously up to $100,000) plus a new invite-only 'AWS Credits for AI Startups' ($200,000+); a new Kiro Startup Credits track (up to 1 year of Kiro Pro+, 1/10/30 users) was added. The stale /free/offers page still shows old $1,000-$100,000 figures and 7-10 day processing, inconsistent with the current credits page ($5,000/$200,000, 5-10 days) — treat the credits page as authoritative. (5) WebSearch was unavailable during this run, so no third-party press confirmation of announcement dates was possible; all claims above are from the official AWS pages fetched today.
- **2025–26 change:** 1) Amazon Pinpoint sunset (HARD DATE): AWS ends support for Amazon Pinpoint on October 30, 2026 — console access and all Pinpoint resources (endpoints, segments, campaigns, journeys, analytics) terminate that day; only the messaging-channel APIs (SMS, voice, mobile push, OTP, phone number validate) continue under the rebranded "AWS End User Messaging" service. Anyone relying on Pinpoint journeys/campaigns must migrate before 2026-10-30. 2) Legacy per-service Free Tier replaced: the old model — e.g., "SES: 3,000 emails/month free for the first 12 months, then $0.10/1K" and "SNS: 100 SMS/month free, US regions" — is no longer published; as of the October 2026 sign-up experience, new accounts instead get up to $200 in Free Tier credits over 6 months (Free plan auto-closes at 6 months or when credits run out). The "then $0.10/1K" figure survives, but as the à-la-carte SES price, while NEW SES accounts default to the more expensive Essentials plan ($0.16/1K) starting July 21, 2026. 3) The formerly permanent "3,000 emails/month free (US regions, no purchase of sending capacity)" SES allowance is not listed on today's official pricing page — treat it as superseded by the credit model for new accounts; legacy account behavior not verifiable (docs pages didn't render). 4) The legacy 100-free-SMS/month US-regions tier also no longer appears on End User Messaging pricing; SMS is now fully pay-per-message. 5) AWS Activate credit ceiling was raised to $200,000 (some pages still show the old $100,000 figure — a 2025-2026 program change, not a sunset).
- **2025–26 change:** 2025-2026 changes (verified against official AWS pages on 2026-10-22): (1) Classic 12-month Free Tier sunset for NEW accounts on July 15, 2025 — replaced by the credit-based Free Plan ($100 + up to $100 earned = up to $200 over 6 months, auto-enrolled, no charges); accounts created before July 15, 2025 remain on legacy 12-month terms (750 hrs/mo t2/t3.micro etc.); legacy terms still published. (2) October 2026: AWS launched a new sign-up experience; the current FAQ now states Free Tier credits expire 6 months from account creation (vs 12 months under the July 2025 Free Tier Terms) and moved credit tracking to the Settings Portal — AWS's own FAQ is internally inconsistent between sections (6 vs 12 months), so treat credit expiry on the new experience as 6 months and verify per-account in the Settings Portal. (3) AWS Activate restructured in 2025-2026: old tiers (Starter $5k / Portfolio up to $100k / YC up to $250k / $5k-$100k sub-tiers) are no longer publicly listed; current structure is Founders ($1,000, up to $5,000 for select participants) + Portfolio (up to $200,000, cap raised from $100,000), with eligibility shifted from 'funded up to Series A' to 'pre-Series B, most recent funding round within the last 12 months', and the application flow now goes through the AWS Builder ID. AWS pages are inconsistent today: /free/offers/ still advertises '$1,000 to $100,000' while /startups/ and the June 4, 2026 AWS blog advertise 'up to $200,000'. (4) No education-only (.edu/student-email) free credits exist at AWS: no free offer requires a .edu or student email — AWS Educate is open to anyone 13/14+ with just an email, the Free Tier is open to any new customer, and AWS Marketplace 'education pricing' is a paid discount, not free. The only university-scoped free offer is AWS Academy (free institution participation + exam vouchers). (5) Free Plan/credits unavailable in China (ZHY, BJS) and GovCloud (US) regions, and not usable via AWS Organizations (auto-upgrades to Paid plan). (6) AWS Educate availability excludes China and Switzerland and raises minimum age in the EU (16) and Algeria/Lebanon/Portugal (18).
- **Critic correction** (AWS Activate (Amazon Bedrock)): Figures are out of date. The Activate Founders self-serve package is $1,000 (select participants can reach $5,000), not about $100. The Portfolio package, for startups tied to an Activate Provider with an Org ID, is now up to $200,000; it was $100,000 before June 2026. Credits can be used on Bedrock third-party models. AI startups ready to scale can talk to an account manager about more.
- **Critic correction** (AWS Startups): Range is outdated. Current tiers are Activate Founders at $1,000 (up to $5,000 for select startups) and Activate Portfolio at up to $200,000 (raised from $100,000 in June 2026). The top of the range should read $200,000 for Portfolio, not $100,000+.
- **Critic correction** (Up to ~$100K in AWS credits (Portfolio/Partner tier; ~$100 Founders self-serve)): The current official maximum is up to $200,000 for the Portfolio tier (requires a provider Org ID; pre-Series B). The Founders (self-funded) tier starts at $1,000, with up to $5,000 for select participants, not about $100.

### Blender

- **Blender 3D suite — 100% free, no license, all uses (students, universities, startups included)** (high) — Blender 5.2.3 LTS (5.3 expected 2026-11-10; also beta, daily/Early Access builds) is free for everyone: GPL (source v2+; binary distributions compatible under v3+), 'Free to Use. Free to Change. Free to Share. Free to Sell Your Work.' No license, registration, account, credit card, or internet required. Output work is the user's sole property. Foundation commitment: 'keep Blender free and open for everyone, forever.' Platforms: Windows x64/ARM, macOS (Apple Silicon), Linux, Steam, source code. Startups and companies get the same free use as students — there is no commercial 'seat' cost at all.
  - *Eligibility:* Universal. No .edu or student email, no proof of status, no region limits, no org size cap. Universities can deploy it on unlimited machines free of charge. This is the entire 'student offer': nothing to discount because there is no license to buy.
  - *How to claim:* Download directly from https://www.blender.org/download/ — no signup, no payment, no trial. Daily builds at builder.blender.org. Nothing to claim or renew.
  - *Expiry:* Never — perpetual free access; version updates are also free. Renewal not applicable.
  - *Gotchas:* Free tier vs paid ecosystem: the app is free, but Blender Marketplace add-ons/assets are paid, and Blender Studio (see next finding) is a paid subscription that names students as an audience — do not confuse the free app with paid services. Optional donation via fund.blender.org supports development but is not required.
  - *Source:* https://www.blender.org/about/license/
- **education.blender.org — free open-licensed education resources for teachers and students** (high) — Blender Foundation-backed community site offering: 'Education Recipes' (lesson plans/curricula, e.g. 'Modeling & animation for absolute beginners', 'VR Class'), 'Quests' (step-by-step tutorials: Party Monkey, House, Rocket, Maze, Chair, Landscape, etc.), 'Concepts' material, and 'Education Badges' — a tiered 7-discipline skill framework (Modeling, Animation, 2D Art, Sculpting, Rigging, Lighting/Camera/Rendering, Video Sequencer, 3-4 levels each). All content released under CC BY-SA 4.0 (or later); source repo is open at projects.blender.org/blender/blender-education-docs. Site framing: 'unrestricted access to high-quality tools without financial barriers or privacy concerns.'
  - *Eligibility:* Open to all teachers and students; no account, no .edu verification, no fee, no region limit. Educator support via chat channel #blender-education on chat.blender.org.
  - *How to claim:* Browse/download directly at education.blender.org; contribution via its 'Get Involved / Contribute' pages. No application.
  - *Expiry:* Perpetual — CC BY-SA license; no sunset date.
  - *Gotchas:* This is curriculum/reference material, not a software license or certification — badges are a self-reference framework, not graded credentials. It complements the free software rather than adding to it.
  - *Source:* https://education.blender.org/
- **Blender Studio (studio.blender.org) — official paid training/production platform; NOT a free offer; no student discount (gotcha)** (high) — Paid subscription funding the Studio team's films and training. Individual plan: €17/month, or €34.50 per quarter (€11.50/month equivalent, 'Save €5.50/month'). Team plan: from €500/month, unlimited seats on a company email domain, up to '8 hours of dedicated training' on higher tiers. Includes: full production-asset access, Open Movie production logs, unlimited trainings (courses, workshops, docs, production lessons), commenting. Payment: major credit cards, PayPal, direct bank transfer. Fees are non-refundable. cloud.blender.org 301-redirects to studio.blender.org — there is no separate free 'Blender Cloud' rendering service.
  - *Eligibility:* The individual plan is explicitly marketed 'for freelancers, students, and instructors' — but at standard price: no free tier, no trial, no student/education discount, no startup program. Students pay the same €17/month.
  - *How to claim:* Join at studio.blender.org/join/ (account via OAuth login required) or /teams/ for the team plan. No free claim path exists.
  - *Expiry:* Subscription cancels per terms; content access ends with the subscription.
  - *Gotchas:* Most likely thing to misread as a student offer because 'students' is in the audience line — it is a paid plan with no concession. The free software itself (finding 1) already covers students at zero cost; Studio adds the Foundation's film production content only. Display bug seen on the studio homepage ('€1150/month') — /join/ page is authoritative: €17/mo.
  - *Source:* https://studio.blender.org/join/

### Canva Pro for Students

- **Canva Free (free tier, open to all students)** (high) — Official US pricing snapshot 2026-10-22: US$0/year. Includes drag-and-drop editor with 1,000+ design types, 1.6M+ templates, 4.7M+ photos/videos/graphics/audio, 1 Brand Kit (3 colors), 5GB cloud storage, ad insights/AI ad creation, access to Affinity, and a shared AI allowance of up to 200 Standard AI uses or 20 Premium AI uses (new 'AI Pass' era layout; older layout shows 'Limited AI access'). Higher-ed students whose school lacks Canva for Campus land on exactly this tier. Prices vary by country; today's page renders localized currency.
  - *Eligibility:* Open to everyone — no student verification, no .edu email, no credit card, no region limit. Sign up at canva.com with email/Google/Apple/Facebook/Microsoft.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Go to canva.com or canva.com/education/students -> 'Sign up for free'. 2) Create an account. 3) Use the Free tier; optionally log in with your university email in case your institution has Canva for Campus (upgrades you to premium automatically).
  - *Expiry:* Indefinite free tier; no renewal. Content persists; premium assets/templates become locked/watermarked if a paid or campus license is lost.
  - *Gotchas:* Free tier excludes the 141M+ premium asset library, 3.6M premium templates, background remover, social scheduler, and paid-tier AI allowance. A 'free' university sign-in only gets Pro features if the institution actually bought Campus. AI allowance on Free is ~200 Standard / 20 Premium uses per month.
  - *Source:* https://www.canva.com/education/students/
- **Canva Education (K-12 license): 100% free Pro features** (high) — Official: 'Canva Education will remain 100% free for K-12 educators, their students, as well as qualified school districts and institutions' — no paywalls, available worldwide, not restricted to any country. Includes most premium features (100M+ premium photos/videos/graphics/fonts, Brand Kits, background remover, Magic tools), plus educator features: LMS integrations (Canvas, Google Classroom, Schoology, Blackboard, D2L, Moodle, MS Teams), SSO/Clever/ClassLink, enterprise-grade security, class management. Free delivery on print orders. Canva states it never uses student data to train AI models.
  - *Eligibility:* K-12 (primary/secondary) only: certified active K-12 teachers, school librarians, learning-support staff, curriculum specialists, tech/vocational K-12 teachers, and government-recognized accredited K-12 schools/districts/education departments — and the students they enroll. Explicitly EXCLUDED: colleges/universities, college/university students, tutors, public librarians, unlicensed homeschoolers, parents, early-stage educational startups. Verified education email = instant access; otherwise upload teacher license/cert/school ID — reviewed within 48h (US/JP/AU/CA/NZ/UK/KR teachers need only a teaching license copy). No credit card required.
  - *How to claim:* Teachers: sign up at canva.com/education with a verified K-12 school email, or upload proof (48h review). Students: ask the teacher to invite you to their Canva class, or sign up with your school email / district SSO — if it matches a school district you get instant Canva Education access; else enter the teacher's email to request access. You cannot self-verify as a K-12 student.
  - *Expiry:* Lasts while you are active and eligible; all Canva Education users are prompted to re-verify credentials every 3 years. Students who leave the class or graduate revert to the free plan — copy designs to a personal account first.
  - *Gotchas:* Premium features can be disabled by school administrators. Access is class/team-scoped, not a personal permanent Pro account: on graduation the account reverts to Canva Free. Intended for educational use — commercial licensing differs from Pro. Verified-email domains must be recognized Canva education domains; personal Gmail/Yahoo not accepted.
  - *Source:* https://www.canva.com/education/eligibility-guidelines/
- **Canva for Campus (higher education): free premium student access via institutional license** (high) — Official: Canva Campus = Canva Enterprise plan 'plus free premium student access included' — all premium student seats free, priced like Enterprise at special higher-ed pricing (no public price; contact sales). Unlock condition: institution purchases a minimum number of staff/faculty licenses calculated from its full-time student body (a Canva consultant sets the threshold; <25 seats -> Canva Business, no HE pricing). Included: SSO/SCIM, FERPA/SOC 2/GDPR compliance, 1TB storage, LMS integrations (Blackboard, Canvas, D2L, Moodle), brand kits, admin AI on/off controls, and per the page, access to Affinity and Flourish within Campus. 'Free access for all students... as part of Canva Step Two' (every enrolled student gets full premium from day one at no cost to them). Examples: UT Austin rolled out free Canva Pro to all students 2025-10-17 (EID sign-in, ~800 sign-ups in first 30 min); Lehigh entered a campus-wide agreement March 2026 with pro-rated rebates for students who paid individually; HKU ran a student-society campaign 2025-11-06 to 2026-11-05 at HK$35/year (~96% below the HK$960 list price).
  - *Eligibility:* Enrolled students of universities/colleges that have purchased Canva Campus (or Enterprise on the path to it). No individual application, no .edu verification, no credit card. Excluded: student employees, part-time/full-time staff and interns of the institution. Not available to individuals at non-adopting schools — they fall back to Canva Free or pay full Pro price.
  - *How to claim:* Student at an adopting campus: sign in to Canva with your university email / campus SSO (or use the institution's Canva sign-in page, e.g. canva sign-in for UT EID); if the campus has Campus you automatically get premium. Otherwise: there is nothing to claim individually — the university's IT/student union must procure the plan ('let your IT team or student union know you want it - student demand is often what gets Campus started').
  - *Expiry:* Lasts while enrolled; on graduation you may lose the school email and with it the free account — copy designs to a personal account. Institutions re-provision via SSO/SCIM; de-provisioning is automatic.
  - *Gotchas:* Institution-paid but admin-gated: faculty/IT can switch AI tools off per course/role/cohort. No public price; 'minimum seat thresholds apply'. If your school has no Campus you pay consumer Pro (US$120-180/yr snapshots) — no individual student discount listed officially in 2026. Individual campus campaigns (HKU HK$35/yr) are local student-union deals, not a Canva-wide student program; the HKU offer window closed 2026-11-05.
  - *Source:* https://www.canva.com/higher-education/
- **Canva Pro / Canva Business 30-day free trial (any user, incl. students)** (medium) — Official help center: Pro and Business offer a free trial, started via 'Start a free trial' on the pricing page; auto-renews into the chosen monthly/yearly plan unless cancelled; trial AI allowance equals the Free tier's (the paid 10x/20x AI allowance only kicks in after conversion); one trial per Canva account AND one per payment method ('if you've used a trial before, you won't see the free trial option; try a new payment method'); trial end date visible under Settings > Billing & Teams with email reminder; 'If you started a free trial without providing payment details, your trial ends automatically and you won't be charged' (a no-card flow exists; other flows/regions require card or PayPal to activate). Third-party 2026 sources describe the standard US trial as 30 days of full Pro (some regions 14 days). After cancel: account reverts to Free, designs retained but premium assets re-watermarked. Paid US list prices (2026 snapshots, transition in progress): Pro US$120-180/yr, Business US$200-250/yr per person, 'Save 16%' on yearly.
  - *Eligibility:* Anyone, not student-specific. No student status needed; no credit card strictly required (a payment-less trial flow exists, though most activations collect card/PayPal up front). Available where Canva accepts payment; trial window is region-dependent.
  - *How to claim:* 1) canva.com/pricing -> 'Start a free trial' under Pro or Business. 2) Log in / create account. 3) Choose monthly or yearly (this only sets what you're charged at conversion). 4) Choose payment method (or the no-card flow if offered). 5) Confirm. 6) Cancel before day 30 via Settings > Billing > Cancel plan to avoid the charge.
  - *Expiry:* 30 days in most regions (14 days in some); ends automatically if no payment details were provided, else converts to paid automatically on the anniversary date.
  - *Gotchas:* Trial-vs-free trap: auto-renews and charges the full chosen plan; the elevated 10x/20x AI allowance is NOT available during the trial (only Free-tier allowance). One trial per account and per payment method — switching card does not reset it. Charges may appear as Canva / Canva*invoice_id / APPLE*Canva / GOOGLE*Canva (app-store billing if subscribed via mobile). New 2026 add-ons (AI Pass monthly add-on; 30-day AI top-ups for Business) cost extra and are not part of the trial.
  - *Source:* https://www.canva.com/help/upgrade-to-canva-pro-or-business/
- **Canva for Startups — no free offer exists** (high) — canva.com/startups is a marketing/resource hub only ('Design your success. Canva is the all-in-one content collaboration platform...'): startup template/pitch-deck content, video tips, a 4-part growth series, and guidance to standard plans ('If there's more than one person, Canva Business is the best plan for startups... pricing tiers to fit every startup's budget, no matter which round of funding you're in'). No discount, free seats, trial credits, or funding/age caps for startups. Canva's eligibility guidelines also explicitly list 'early stage start-ups with an educational focus' as NOT eligible for Canva Education. Related adjacent program (not student/startup): Canva Nonprofits — 100% free Pro features + team tools for registered nonprofits/NGOs, one team of up to 50 users, extra seats 50% off; government agencies, political orgs, educational institutions and for-profits excluded.
  - *Eligibility:* None — no startup-specific eligibility because no startup offer exists. (Nonprofit program: registered 501(c)(3)-equivalent orgs, up to 50 free users.)
  - *How to claim:* N/A — nothing to claim. A startup can only use Canva Free, the standard Pro/Business trial, or the nonprofit program if it is a registered charity.
  - *Expiry:* N/A
  - *Gotchas:* Don't confuse the 'Canva for Startups' content hub with a startup discount program. Also 'Canva Teams is no longer available for new sign-ups or upgrades' (2026) — team features now live under Canva Business.
  - *Source:* https://www.canva.com/startups/
- **2025–26 change:** The known '~50% off Canva Pro first year for verified students (~$49.99/yr vs $99.99 via .edu or national student verification)' does NOT appear on any official Canva page as of 2026-10-22: the students page offers only Canva Free (higher ed) or free K-12 Canva Education, the eligibility guidelines explicitly exclude college/university students from free Canva Education, and the pricing page lists no student price. The individual ~50% student Pro plan appears to have been sunset and replaced by two structural programs: (1) free Canva Education for K-12, and (2) Canva for Campus — where universities buy Enterprise-priced licenses and all premium student seats are free (e.g. UT Austin, free since 2025-10-17; Lehigh, March 2026; HKU student-union campaign HK$35/yr valid 2025-11-06 to 2026-11-05). US Pro's list price is also no longer $99.99: 2026 pages show US$120-180/yr (a transition-era A/B or recent increase; one third-party check recorded $144/yr on 2026-10-16). 2025-2026 material changes: Canva Pro trials now grant only the Free-tier AI allowance during the trial, plans auto-renew, Canva Teams was discontinued for new sign-ups, and new paid add-ons (AI Pass monthly add-on; 30-day AI top-ups for Business) were introduced. Note: canva.com blocks WebFetch (403); official pages were verified today via direct curl. Local university campaigns (HKU etc.) are institution-specific and expire on their own dates, not Canva policy.

### Cloudflare

- **Workers AI free tier: 10,000 free Neurons/day (no signup offer needed)** (high) — Cloudflare's Workers Free AND Workers Paid plans each include 10,000 Neurons per day at no charge; the cap resets daily at 00:00 UTC; exceeding it makes further Workers AI requests fail. 72 models currently listed (39 LLM, 6 embeddings, 6 image, 10 audio, 11 other). Pricing page was restructured: "updated to be more granular, with per-model unit-based pricing presented" (per-model $/token, $/tile, $/minute alongside Neuron equivalents); backend billing still uses Neurons. On Workers Paid, usage above the daily allowance costs $0.011 per 1,000 Neurons; prepaid AI Gateway credits can also cover frontier-model usage and raise rate limits for those models.
  - *Eligibility:* Anyone with a Cloudflare account on the Workers Free plan (no credit card, no application, no .edu/funding requirements); the same allowance also ships with Workers Paid. US region: no student-only or startup-only gate.
  - *How to claim:* Create a free Cloudflare account (no payment method required for the Free plan) and call Workers AI (HTTP API or Worker binding). 10,000 Neurons/day auto-applies; nothing to redeem.
  - *Expiry:* Permanent free tier; daily 10k-Neuron window resets at 00:00 UTC. No fixed end date.
  - *Gotchas:* 8 frontier models (kimi-k2.6, kimi-k2.7-code, glm-5.2, glm-5.3, glm-5.3-flash, deepseek-v4-flash-0731, deepseek-v4.1-flash-1017, deepseek-v4-pro-0813) require a paid billing method — unavailable on Free plan alone, even within the free Neuron allowance. Neurons-per-request varies by model and input/output direction, so the effective free daily quota in tokens differs widely per model; per-model caps also exist. On Paid, overage is $0.011/1k Neurons. Egress/data costs: Workers AI calls from a Worker are same-edge, but if your app is outside Workers, standard API call costs apply.
  - *Source:* https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers-ai/platform/pricing/(lastupdatedOct20,2026—readtoday)
- **Cloudflare for Startups: up to $350,000 in credits (3 tiers: $10k / $100k / $350k)** (high) — Current structure (read on cloudflare.com/startups today): Tier 3 = $10k credits (bootstrapped/self-funded or under $1M raised, no partner needed); Tier 2 = $100k (under $5M raised, funded by an affiliated partner, account manager + expert sessions); Tier 1 = $350k ($5M+ raised, funded by affiliated partner, account manager, priority support, bi-weekly Solutions Architect office hours). Credits are valid 1 year or until fully consumed, whichever comes first; no extensions. Credits cover usage-based services; Workers AI is subject to per-tier caps: $2,500 (Tier 3), $10,000 (Tier 2), $50,000 (Tier 1), at standard partner-model pricing; R2 capped at $10,000; AI Gateway temporarily NOT covered by credits. Free at every tier: 3 enterprise domains, unlimited Pro/Business plans, DDoS/bot protection, WAF with 1,000 custom rules, CDN/DNS, LB (2 origins), 100% SLA. Business email matching company domain required; a valid payment method on file is required.
  - *Eligibility:* For-profit tech company incorporated within the last 10 years; funding up to Series B, funded within the last 12 months; live public website; active LinkedIn/X/GitHub; first-time program applicants only; business email matching your company domain (no personal email). Tier 2/1 require funding by an affiliated (approved VC/accelerator) partner.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Create a standard Cloudflare account and add a billing/payment method. 2) Fill the application at /lp/startups (company domain email, website, social link, Cloudflare account ID). 3) Manual review ~48 hours; on approval credits and plan upgrades are applied to the account. Rejections include a reason and you may request re-evaluation.
  - *Expiry:* Credits: 12 months from approval or until consumed; no renewal/extension offered (upgrading tier does not reset the 12-month window). Program expansion (Sept 2025) also covers non-profits/civil-society orgs (up to $250k, per Birthday Week 2025 announcement).
  - *Gotchas:* Credit card/payment method IS required despite being a 'free credits' program. Overage beyond the credit balance is billed automatically. The old 4-tier structure ($5k/$25k/$100k/$250k, announced April 2025) was replaced — Tier 3 raised $5k→$10k, top tier $250k→$350k. Workers AI sub-cap ($2.5k–$50k depending on tier) means most of the $350k is NOT spendable on Workers AI. AI Gateway excluded for now. Non-US regional pages (e.g. en-in) still show the outdated $250k/4-tier structure — the US .com page is authoritative.
  - *Source:* https://www.cloudflare.com/startups/(readtoday)
- **Cloudflare for Students: 12 months of Workers Paid at no cost (US .edu students)** (high) — US students 18+ with a verified .edu billing email get the Workers Paid plan's $5/month base fee waived for 12 months, with increased usage allotments vs. the free plan: up to 500 Worker scripts, 10M requests/month (+$0.30/M after), 20M included logs/month (+$0.60/M after), up to 30s CPU per request, 6,000 build minutes/month, 6 concurrent build slots, extra Workers KV edge storage. The announcement post (Sept 22, 2025) additionally listed Hyperdrive unlimited DB queries/day, Durable Objects 1M req/day (+$0.15/M after), Logpush 10M events/month, and Queues 1M ops/month — usage beyond the paid-tier allotments still incurs standard rates, and Workers AI remains metered at $0.011/1k Neurons (10k Neurons/day included on any plan). Includes a dedicated student Discord. Announced at Birthday Week, Sept 22, 2025.
  - *Eligibility:* United States only; 18+; currently enrolled in an eligible educational program; verified .edu email address that must be the BILLING email (not just signup email). Expansion to other countries is requested via the program's school-domain registration form.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Sign up (or log in) with your verified .edu email. 2) On the Billing page, add a valid credit card and confirm your billing email is the .edu address. 3) Add the Workers Paid plan at the Workers plans page — the $5/mo fee auto-waives for 12 months. 4) Confirmation email arrives in your inbox.
  - *Expiry:* 12 months; if you don't renew to standard Workers Paid, the account auto-reverts to Workers Free with no charge. No automatic renewal of the waiver.
  - *Gotchas:* Credit card IS required (verification + overage billing) even though the fee is waived; overage usage above student allotments is charged to that card. Not stackable with the Startup Program's business email requirement (personal .edu vs company domain). Workers AI 'free' access here is just the plan's standard 10k Neurons/day — the student offer does NOT add extra AI neurons; heavy LLM use costs money on the student account.
  - *Source:* https://www.cloudflare.com/students/(readtoday)+https://blog.cloudflare.com/workers-for-students/(Sept22,2025)
- **Workers Launchpad: cohort accelerator — Startup Enterprise Plan ($250k in credits) + VC access** (medium) — Cohort-based funding program for startups building on the Cloudflare Developer Platform. Benefits per the program page: Startup Enterprise Plan worth $250,000 in credits plus enterprise features (Workers, Workers AI, KV, Durable Objects, R2, Images, Stream, D1, etc.), VC introductions from Cloudflare's partner network (program claims >$2B committed to Workers startups), Workers Founders Bootcamp, weekly office hours, virtual Demo Day with blog feature. Cohort #6 was announced Sept 2025. Note: the live page is heavily JS-rendered; my direct fetch of the page today returned only the high-level landing copy — the $250k benefit text comes from the cached/extracted page content in search results, not a full live re-read.
  - *Eligibility:* Early-stage startups building on Workers/Cloudflare platform; application via cohort-based process (no public age/funding caps published on the page I could fully read; cohort selection at Cloudflare's discretion). Launchpad alumni/participants are also eligible for Cloudflare's 2026 office 'hubs' in SF, Austin, London, Lisbon.
  - *How to claim:* Apply to the next cohort via the Launchpad page/application form; participate in the program; receive credits via the Startup Enterprise Plan, VC intro, bootcamp, and demo day.
  - *Expiry:* Cohort-bound; the underlying $250k credit follows the Startup Program's 1-year validity. New cohorts announced roughly annually.
  - *Gotchas:* Details on credit amounts/caps couldn't be confirmed from a clean live page read today (JS-rendered page returned only landing copy) — treat the $250k figure as medium confidence. Overlaps/stacking rules with the standard Startup Program tiers are not spelled out on the page.
  - *Source:* https://www.cloudflare.com/startups/workers-launchpad/(directfetchtodayreturnedpartialcontent;benefitdetailsfromthepage'scachedextraction)
- **No standalone 'trial credits' or education license for Workers AI inference itself** (medium) — Cloudflare does not publish a separate AI trial-credit pool, per-model free-token grant, or academic/education license for Workers AI inference as of 2026-10-22. The only AI-related free offers are (a) the plan-embedded 10k Neurons/day free tier (finding 1) and (b) Workers AI caps inside the Startup Program ($2.5k–$50k by tier). The only 'credit-adjacent' paid option is prepaid AI Gateway credits, which can route frontier-model Workers AI usage and grant higher rate limits — but prepaid, not free. Offer trackers (e.g. perq.sh) list exactly two tracked Cloudflare offers: the startup credit tiers and the 10k Neurons/day free tier — no trial-credit offer exists. Non-profit/civil-society orgs can receive up to $250k credits via the Sept 2025 Startup Program expansion (announced at Birthday Week 2025; dedicated program page could not be located — likely under the startups portal).
  - *Eligibility:* n/a — absence finding. Non-profit orgs: civil society/public interest orgs (up to $250k credits, incl. AI, databases, security products).
  - *How to claim:* Nothing to claim for trial credits/education license; use the free tier + Startup Program + Student program above. Non-profits apply through the Cloudflare for Startups application portal with org details.
  - *Expiry:* n/a
  - *Gotchas:* Do not confuse 'trial' with the free tier: free tier is permanent but hard-capped (requests fail beyond 10k Neurons/day, not throttled). Prepaid AI Gateway credits are a paid product, not a free grant. The old 'up to $250k, 4 tiers' startup structure (April 2025) is superseded by the $350k 3-tier structure — third-party trackers may still show stale $250k/4-tier data.
  - *Source:* https://perq.sh/vendors/cloudflare(offertracker,checked2026-10-22)+https://blog.cloudflare.com/(BirthdayWeek2025non-profitannouncement,Sept22,2025)
- **Cloudflare for Startups — 3-tier credit program (up to $350,000 in credits, 12 months)** (high) — Official program (https://www.cloudflare.com/startups/, read 2026-10-22). Tiers: $10k credits — bootstrapped/self-funded, under $1M raised, no minimum funding; $100k credits — under $5M raised, must be funded by an affiliated Cloudflare partner (VC/accelerator); $350k credits — $5M+ raised, funded by an affiliated partner. Credits are valid 12 months from the confirmation email or until fully consumed (whichever first); no extensions; non-transferable/non-refundable; overages billed to card on file. Credits cover usage-based services: Workers, Workers for Platforms, Durable Objects, Workflows, Workers AI (sub-caps $2,500 T3 / $10,000 T2 / $50,000 T1), Workers KV, D1, Queues, Vectorize, R2 (sub-cap $10,000), Pages, Images and Stream, Cache Reserve, Argo Smart Routing. Included at no cost on all tiers (not drawn from credits): 3 enterprise domains, unlimited Business and Pro plans, DDoS and bot protection, WAF with 1,000 custom rules, Security Analytics, SSL certs, Page Shield, CDN and DNS, Load Balancing (2 origins), Rate Limiting (1 rule), 100% SLA. Support: priority tickets + chat; T2 adds case-dependent account manager + expert sessions; T1 adds account manager, priority tech support, bi-weekly Solutions Architect office hours. Excluded from credits: AI Gateway (temporarily), registrar fees (domain registration/renewal), enterprise add-ons (some require email request), Magic Transit / Enterprise Spectrum / Dedicated IPs / China Network. Review ~48 hours; contact startups@cloudflare.com; multiple distinct startups can each apply under different account IDs/emails.
  - *Eligibility:* For-profit tech startup; founded within last 10 years (corporate age <=10 yrs); funded up to Series B with funding within last 12 months; actively building a tech product; public verifiable website; active LinkedIn/X/GitHub; first-time applicants only (no re-entry); business email matching your domain required (personal emails rejected); excluded: educational institutions, personal blogs, consultancies, agencies, MSPs, resellers. $100k/$350k tiers additionally require funding by an affiliated Cloudflare partner. No region limit stated (program is global). A valid payment method (credit card) MUST be on file — so CC IS required, unlike the free plan.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Create a Cloudflare account, add payment method, 2) Submit the application via 'Apply now' on cloudflare.com/startups, 3) Wait up to 48h for review, 4) Confirmation email activates the program; credits auto-deduct in dashboard. Existing members on the old $5k/$250k caps may email startups@cloudflare.com for a case-by-case tier-upgrade evaluation (does NOT reset the 12-month expiry).
  - *Expiry:* Credits valid 12 months from confirmation email or until consumed, whichever comes first; no extensions. Program itself is ongoing (long-term).
  - *Gotchas:* CC required on file and overages auto-billed after credits run out; R2 sub-cap $10k and Workers AI sub-caps can bite if you plan to use those; AI Gateway and registrar fees not credit-covered; Enterprise add-ons (e.g., Advanced Rate Limiting, Bot Management, extra LB origins) need manual email support requests; post-graduation Enterprise domains auto-downgrade to Free plan; November 18, 2025 global outage is a single-vendor concentration risk noted by the community; '1,000 WAF rules' marketing applies to program/Enterprise domains, not to the regular Free plan (5 rules).
  - *Source:* https://www.cloudflare.com/startups/
- **Cloudflare Free plan (site plans) + Developer Platform free allowances — permanent, no credit card** (high) — Free site plan: $0/mo — unlimited-bandwidth CDN (335 data centers), unmetered DDoS mitigation, free Universal SSL, 1.1.1.1 DNS, Free WAF Managed Ruleset, 5 WAF custom rules/zone, 1 rate-limiting rule, email obfuscation, hotlink protection. No SLA, ticket-only support. No credit card required for the free plan. Separate permanent per-service free allowances on the pay-as-you-go Developer Platform (cloudflare.com/plans/, read 2026-10-22): Workers 100,000 requests/day + 10ms CPU/request; R2 10 GB-month storage + 1M Class A ops (zero egress fees); Workers KV 1GB / 100k reads/day; D1 5GB / 5M rows read/day; Queues 10k ops/day; Workers AI 10,000 neurons/day; Vectorize 30M queried dims/month; Workers Logs 200k events/day; Containers 25 GiB-hrs memory + 1TB egress; Browser Rendering 10 min/day; TURN/SFU 1,000 GB/mo egress; Images 5,000 transformations/mo. Paid Developer Platform rates apply above allowances ($0.30/M Worker requests, $0.02/M CPU ms, etc.) — no card needed until you exceed allowances on a paid component.
  - *Eligibility:* Anyone; no signup gate, no .edu, no funding requirement. Free site plan needs no payment method; usage-based overage billing activates when you exceed free Developer Platform allowances.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Sign up at dash.cloudflare.com (free), 2) Add a domain (zone) for CDN/DDoS/SSL/WAF-free-managed-ruleset, 3) Deploy Workers/Pages/R2/etc. free; overages metered at pay-as-you-go rates.
  - *Expiry:* Permanent free tier; per-service allowances current as of 2026-10-22 pricing page.
  - *Gotchas:* WAF custom rules on Free = 5 (not 1,000 — that's the Enterprise/startup-program number); Free plan excludes SLA, advanced bot management, image optimization, China network; CDN ToS forbids using free CDN for video streaming/large-file hosting (512MB per-file cache cap); no SSO/log retention beyond 24h (Zero Trust free tier = 50 users).
  - *Source:* https://www.cloudflare.com/plans/free/
- **Cloudflare for Students — 12 months of the paid Workers plan free for .edu students (launched Birthday Week, Sept 22, 2025)** (high) — 12-month free access to the paid developer features plan (Workers Paid, normally $5/mo, fee waived): deploy up to 500 Worker scripts; 10M requests/month (then $0.30/M); 20M included log events/month (then $0.60/M); up to 30s CPU per request; 6,000 included build minutes/month ($0.005/min after); 6 concurrent build slots; extra Workers KV edge storage; all free-plan features; dedicated student Discord community. After 12 months the account auto-reverts to the Free plan with no charge; renew by upgrading to Workers Paid at standard pricing. Offers may not be redeemed multiple times; Cloudflare may use your name/project in marketing and can discontinue the program without notice; no 24/7 enterprise phone support or SLA. Launched Sept 22, 2025 as 'the first step for Cloudflare for Students', currently US-only; Cloudflare is adding other schools' domains on request.
  - *Eligibility:* Students in the United States only, at least 18 years old, currently enrolled in an eligible educational program, with a verified .edu email (required and must be set as the billing email). A valid credit card MUST be on file (base $5/mo fee waived; you are billed for usage above base allotments). No .edu = no access; international students must apply to have their institution's domain added.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Sign up for a free Cloudflare account with your .edu email and verify it, 2) On the Billing page add a valid credit card and confirm billing email = your .edu, 3) On the Workers Plans page add the Workers Paid plan (fee auto-waived for 12 months), 4) Wait for the confirmation email that the account redeemed the offer.
  - *Expiry:* 12 months per redemption, not renewable free — reverts to Free plan; renew only by paying for Workers Paid. No set end date for the program; US-only at launch, expansion pending.
  - *Gotchas:* CC required (card on file; overages billed at standard usage rates even during the free year); usage caps differ from the Startup program sub-caps — over-limit workers requests cost $0.30/M; not available to students outside the US yet; marketing usage of your name; program can be changed/discontinued without notice.
  - *Source:* https://www.cloudflare.com/students/
- **The Cold Start pitch competition — $500,000 credits prize (2026 edition, now concluded)** (high) — One-off annual competition announced Sept 28, 2026: five early-stage startups each got 5 minutes on stage at Cloudflare Connect (Moscone West, San Francisco) on Oct 19, 2026; judges (Matthew Prince, Michelle Zatlyn, Dane Knecht) picked a winner receiving $500,000 in Cloudflare credits, a San Francisco billboard, and a VIP speakers dinner; finalist flights covered. Target: early-stage startups in the US and Canada with <$10M raised.
  - *Eligibility:* Early-stage startup, based in US or Canada, raised less than $10M. Application window closed Oct 2, 2026.
  - *How to claim:* Applied via the form linked on the blog post (closed). 2027 edition not yet announced; watch blog.cloudflare.com under the Cloudflare for Startups tag.
  - *Expiry:* 2026 round closed (event held Oct 19, 2026 — before today, Oct 22, 2026); likely recurring annually but no next date published.
  - *Gotchas:* Prize is winner-only (4 of 5 finalists get no credits); not a general program — do not plan budgeting around it.
  - *Source:* https://blog.cloudflare.com/introducing-the-cold-start/
- **Cloudflare Workers Free plan ($0/mo, no credit card)** (high) — $0/month. Core Worker: 100,000 requests/day; 10 ms CPU time per invocation, no charge for duration; max 100 Worker scripts per account (500 on paid); 128 MB memory per isolate; static assets free and unlimited requests (up to 20,000 files per Worker version, 25 MiB per file); no charge for data transfer (egress/bandwidth). Free included data stores on the same plan: KV (100,000 reads/day, 1,000 writes/day, 1 GB), D1 (5 GB total, 5M row reads/day, 100K row writes/day), R2 (10 GB-month, 1M Class A ops/mo, 10M Class B ops/mo, free egress), Durable Objects (100,000 requests/day, 13,000 GB-s/day), Queues (10,000 ops/day), Workflows (100,000 req/day, 10 ms CPU, 1 GB-month storage), Hyperdrive (100,000 queries/day), Vectorize (5M stored dimensions, 30M queried dimensions/mo), Workers Logs (200,000 events/day, 3-day retention). Containers: N/A on Free. Paid Workers plan starts at $5/mo (10M requests/mo, +$0.30/M; 30M CPU ms/mo, +$0.02/M CPU ms; unlimited egress).
  - *Eligibility:* Any person/company — create a free Cloudflare account at dash.cloudflare.com. No .edu email required, no regional restriction, no startup age/funding criteria, and no credit card required (Cloudflare's developer-facing messaging: 'Start building for free — no credit card required'). No application or approval.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Sign up for a free Cloudflare account. 2) Go to Workers & Pages in the dashboard, or deploy locally with `npx wrangler deploy`. 3) Free plan is the default — no selection or billing setup needed. Limits apply automatically; exceeding them triggers the pay-per-use option ($5/mo minimum) only if you add a billing profile.
  - *Expiry:* No expiry/sunset date for the free plan; daily quotas reset at 00:00 UTC. Ongoing as of 2026-10-22 (pricing page last updated 2026-10-02; limits page 2026-10-12).
  - *Gotchas:* 10 ms CPU per invocation is a hard cap on Free (long jobs need Paid at 5 min cap); 100,000 requests is a per-DAY cap (≈3.1M/mo max), not monthly; static-asset 'unlimited' is bounded by 20,000 files/25 MiB per file; Durable Objects on Free are SQLite-only (no container DOs); Workers Logs free tier (200K events/day) migrates to Cloudflare Observability pricing on 2026-12-01; Durable Objects SQLite storage billing begins Jan 2026; no-charge-for-egress applies to CDN delivery, but connecting metered third-party services can incur charges on their side.
  - *Source:* https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers/platform/pricing/(limits:https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers/platform/limits/)
- **Cloudflare Pages free plan (frontend hosting)** (high) — $0/month, available on the Free plan: 500 builds/month, 1 concurrent build, 20-minute build timeout; 100 projects (sites) per account (limit not routinely increased); up to 20,000 files per site, 25 MiB per file; 100 custom domains per project; unlimited preview deployments; unlimited users; no egress charges; no credit card required. New-project creation is rate-limited during the first 48h of account age.
  - *Eligibility:* Any Cloudflare account; no .edu, no credit card, no regional restriction. Domain must use Cloudflare nameservers for custom domains.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Free account at dash.cloudflare.com. 2) Connect a Git repo (GitHub/GitLab) under Pages or upload manually; build+deploy is automatic. No application.
  - *Expiry:* No expiry; 500 builds reset monthly. Current as of 2026-09-05 (last updated on limits page).
  - *Gotchas:* Pages is in a migration/maintenance posture: Cloudflare docs actively direct users to Workers static assets ('Workers supports most Pages use cases and offers a broader feature set'; a dedicated 'Migrate From Pages' guide exists, updated 2026-10-12), so new features are landing on Workers, not Pages. Pages has no published aggregate storage cap (file-count cap instead of MB cap). To host >100 sites, Cloudflare pushes Workers for Platforms (paid product).
  - *Source:* https://developers.cloudflare.com/pages/platform/limits/
- **R2 free tier (object storage with free egress)** (high) — Free every month: 10 GB-month storage, 1 million Class A operations, 10 million Class B operations, and egress is free (R2 charges no egress at any tier). Paid Standard: $0.015/GB-month, $4.50/M Class A, $0.36/M Class B. Added 2026: R2 SQL free tier 10 GB data-scanned/mo (billing enabled 2026-08-03); R2 Data Catalog free tier 1M catalog ops + 10 GB compaction/mo (billing enabled 2026-08-03).
  - *Eligibility:* Any free Cloudflare account; no credit card required; no .edu or regional limit.
  - *How to claim:* Create an R2 bucket in the dashboard (or via `wrangler r2`); free tier is automatic — no application.
  - *Expiry:* No expiry; monthly free allowances reset. Verified on R2 pricing page last updated 2026-10-01.
  - *Gotchas:* Free tier applies to Standard storage only — Infrequent Access storage is NOT covered; R2 SQL minimum 10 MB scanned per query; unauthorized (401/403) requests are not billed; connecting R2 to other metered services may be charged by those services.
  - *Source:* https://developers.cloudflare.com/r2/pricing/
- **D1 free tier (serverless SQLite)** (high) — Free: 5 GB total storage, 5,000,000 row reads/day, 100,000 row writes/day, $0. Paid: first 25B rows/mo reads, 50M rows/mo writes, first 5 GB included, then $0.75/GB-mo storage.
  - *Eligibility:* Any free Cloudflare account; no credit card; no .edu or regional restriction.
  - *How to claim:* `wrangler d1 create my-db` or dashboard; included automatically in the Workers Free plan.
  - *Expiry:* No expiry; daily quotas reset at 00:00 UTC. Limits as of pricing page updated 2026-10-02.
  - *Gotchas:* Quotas are per-DAY, not per-month (daily reads cap = up to 150M/mo, writes = 3M/mo); D1 is designed for Workers bindings — it adds no egress cost.
  - *Source:* https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers/platform/pricing/
- **Durable Objects free tier (stateful coordination)** (high) — Free: 100,000 requests/day and 13,000 GB-s of duration/day; SQLite-backed storage is the only DO storage option on the Free plan. Paid: 1M requests/mo (+$0.15/M), 400,000 GB-s/mo (+$12.50/M GB-s).
  - *Eligibility:* Any free Cloudflare account; no credit card; no .edu or regional restriction.
  - *How to claim:* Deploy a Worker with a DO class via `wrangler deploy`; free allowances automatic.
  - *Expiry:* No expiry; daily reset at 00:00 UTC.
  - *Gotchas:* Containers-backed DOs are NOT available on Free (listed N/A) — container DO billing is on the $5/mo paid plan; DO SQLite storage billing began January 2026 (target 2026-01-07 per changelog on the pricing page).
  - *Source:* https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers/platform/pricing/
- **Workers KV free tier** (high) — Free: 100,000 key reads/day, 1,000 writes/deletes/lists/day, 1 GB stored data; limits reset daily at 00:00 UTC. Paid: 10M reads/mo (+$0.50/M), 1M writes (+$5.00/M), 1 GB (+$0.50/GB-mo).
  - *Eligibility:* Any free Cloudflare account; no credit card; no .edu or regional restriction.
  - *How to claim:* Create a KV namespace in the dashboard / `wrangler kv`; automatic.
  - *Expiry:* No expiry; daily reset. KV legacy API routes (/accounts/.../workers/namespaces/*) are deprecated — migrate to /storage/kv/namespaces/* before 2026-10-15.
  - *Gotchas:* The write cap (1,000/day) is the binding constraint for most apps; legacy API route deprecation deadline 2026-10-15 (past due as of 2026-10-22, so use the new routes).
  - *Source:* https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers/platform/pricing/
- **Cloudflare Pages Free plan (free tier)** (high) — $0/mo, permanently free, no application. Static hosting: unlimited requests and unlimited bandwidth ('Requests to static assets are free and unlimited' — Workers platform pricing doc). Monthly/structural limits: 500 builds (deploys) per month, 1 concurrent build, 20-minute build timeout; up to 100 projects per account (limit 'not routinely increased'; new-project creation throttled for first 48h of a new account); 100 custom domains per project (free; Pro 250, Business/Enterprise 500); 20,000 files per project on Free (paid plans get 100,000 via env var PAGES_WRANGLER_MAJOR_VERSION=4); 25 MiB max per file; unlimited preview deployments; unlimited dashboard users; _headers 100 rules (2,000 chars/value); _redirects 2,100 total (2,000 static + 100 dynamic). No region limits stated in the limits doc. Docs last updated Sep 5, 2026; overview doc also states '500 deploys per month on the Free plan'.
  - *Eligibility:* Anyone with a Cloudflare account; no .edu, no company, no approval. No credit card required for the free plan in practice (dashboard sign-up is cardless) — this is not explicitly stated on the docs pages I read today, so treat the no-card sub-claim as medium confidence; the official limits/pricing docs simply never gate the Free plan on payment.
  - *How to claim:* Create a free account at dash.cloudflare.com (no card), then deploy a Pages project via Git integration, C3 CLI, or direct upload. Project lands on the Free plan automatically; add a custom domain in the project's domains tab if needed.
  - *Expiry:* Permanent free tier, no expiration or renewal. Quotas reset monthly (builds) or daily (Workers-side quotas).
  - *Gotchas:* (1) Pages Functions are billed as Workers: free Workers plan = 100,000 requests/day and 10 ms CPU time per invocation, plus free quotas for KV (100k reads/1k writes, 1 GB), D1, Queues, DO — functions that exceed these fall under the paid $5/mo minimum or overage rates. Static-asset traffic stays free and unlimited regardless. (2) Large assets (>25 MiB) don't fit — use R2 public buckets instead. (3) 100-project cap is effectively a hard limit; beyond that Cloudflare points to Workers for Platforms. (4) Strategic shift 2025–2026: Pages was folded into the Workers platform — docs now say 'Start new projects with Workers' and 'Workers supports most Pages use cases'; Pages itself is not being sunset but new features land in Workers. (5) Paid plan column in the limits doc (Pro/Business) corresponds to the Workers platform pricing (from $5/mo min) — Pages alone has no standalone paid subscription; '100 custom domains/project' on Free is per-project, multiplied across up to 100 projects.
  - *Source:* https://developers.cloudflare.com/pages/platform/limits/;https://developers.cloudflare.com/pages/;https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers/platform/pricing/
- **Cloudflare for Startups (startup credit program)** (high) — Official program page read today: up to $350,000 in credits for early-stage companies, tiered: Tier 3 = $10,000 credits (bootstrapped/self-funded, under $1M raised, no minimum funding, benefits: community/Discord/docs); Tier 2 = $100,000 (under $5M raised, funded by an affiliated partner; account manager case-dependent + expert sessions); Tier 1 = $350,000 ($5M+ raised, funded by an affiliated partner; account manager, priority support, bi-weekly Solutions Architect office hours). Credits cover usage-based services incl. Pages: Workers, Workers for Platforms, Durable Objects, Workflows, Workers AI, KV, D1, Queues, Vectorize, R2 (capped $10,000), Pages, Images and Stream, Cache Reserve, Argo Smart Routing. Product caps: Workers AI $2,500 (T3)/$10,000 (T2)/$50,000 (T1); R2 $10,000; AI Gateway temporarily NOT covered. Included at no cost in all tiers: 3 enterprise domains, unlimited Business and Pro plans, DDoS/bot protection, WAF with 1,000 custom rules, SSL, CDN+DNS, LB (2 origins), Rate Limiting (1 rule), 100% SLA. Excluded from credits: registrar fees, Enterprise add-ons, Magic Transit, Enterprise Spectrum, Dedicated IPs, China Network.
  - *Eligibility:* ALL must be met: for-profit business; incorporated/founded within the last 10 years; funded up to Series B (within last 12 months); actively building a technology product; publicly accessible live website; public presence on LinkedIn, X or GitHub; first-time applicants only; business email matching your domain (no personal email). Explicitly excluded: educational institutions, personal blogs, consultancies, service-based agencies, MSPs, resellers. No .edu (students use the free plan instead). No geographic restriction stated (China Network excluded and needs a dedicated contract).
  - *How to claim:* Apply via the landing page linked from cloudflare.com/startups (form at /lp/startups): fill out the application describing product and team; review typically within 48 hours; activation confirmation by email; questions to startups@cloudflare.com. A valid payment method must be uploaded on file as part of the program.
  - *Expiry:* Credits valid 1 year or until fully consumed, whichever comes first; Cloudflare states it cannot approve extensions. After expiration, the credit card on file is billed for usage unless a prepaid contract is signed.
  - *Gotchas:* Credits are for usage-based services only — the free Pages plan is $0 to begin with, so the program only pays for your overage/paid-plan consumption; 'no cost' bundle (3 enterprise domains, WAF, SLA) is the real structural win. CREDIT CARD / payment method IS required (unlike the free plan). Funding caps matter: Tier 1 needs an affiliated-partner round, not just any $5M+. The older regional sub-pages (en-gb/en-ca /forstartups/) still show the pre-2026 four-tier $5k/$25k/$100k/$250k structure — the main cloudflare.com/startups page is authoritative.
  - *Source:* https://www.cloudflare.com/startups/
- **Student / education offer** (medium) — No student-specific free offer exists for Pages or the Cloudflare developer platform. The official education page (read today) lists no academic license, no student credits, and no .edu-based benefit; the only quantified education program is 'Project Cybersafe Schools': free cloud email security and DNS filtering (CIPA-compliant) for small US K-12 public school districts — a security offering, not hosting, and not available to individual students/educators. Cloudflare's startup program explicitly excludes educational institutions. Individual students are served by the universal $0 Pages Free plan (no card, no application), which is the same as the general public's free tier; free developer courses/community exist but carry no hosting entitlement.
  - *Eligibility:* Students: no dedicated eligibility — they use the general Free plan (any account, no card). Project Cybersafe Schools: small US public K-12 districts. No .edu requirement anywhere; .edu email grants no special Cloudflare benefit.
  - *How to claim:* Nothing to claim for students beyond the standard free plan. For K-12 districts: contact Cloudflare education team via the contact form on cloudflare.com/education.
  - *Expiry:* N/A — absence of a student program; free plan is permanent.
  - *Gotchas:* Search budget in this session was exhausted, so I could not exhaustively rule out ad-hoc student partnerships (e.g., accelerator/course-specific credit drops like the historical Workers Launchpad, which is partner-gated, not student-gated). Confidence is medium: I read the official education page today but student programs, if any, could live under partner/launchpad names I didn't fetch.
  - *Source:* https://www.cloudflare.com/education/
- **Cloudflare Free Plan (CDN + DDoS + DNS + basic WAF)** (high) — Perpetual $0/month free tier. Includes global CDN, always-on unlimited DDoS mitigation (no configuration), unlimited DNS zones/queries, TLS certificates, and basic WAF + Bot Fight Mode on the free tier. Runs on 100+ data centers across 330+ cities. Bundles the Workers Free compute tier and per-service free allowances (R2 10 GB, KV 1 GB, D1 5 GB, Durable Objects, Hyperdrive).
  - *Eligibility:* Open to anyone (students, startups, individuals, all regions). No .edu email, no age/funding cap. A credit card is NOT required: a Free account is created with just email + password.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Sign up at dash.cloudflare.com/sign-up with email + password (no CC). 2) Add your domain via CNAME or point name servers to Cloudflare. 3) CDN + DDoS protection auto-activate for free.
  - *Expiry:* No expiry — active for the life of the account.
  - *Gotchas:* No CC needed to start, but adding a payment method unlocks paid products and any overage/egress beyond the free allowances is billed. Free plan has per-feature caps (e.g. Pages 500 builds/mo and 100 custom domains; 512 MB CDN cache).
  - *Source:* https://www.cloudflare.com/plans/
- **Cloudflare Workers Free Plan (Compute)** (high) — $0. 100,000 Worker invocations/day (resets 00:00 UTC), 10 ms CPU per request, up to 100 Workers, 64 MiB memory, 50 external + 1,000 Cloudflare-service subrequests per invocation. Free allowances also include KV 1 GB, R2 10 GB, D1 5 GB, Durable Objects, Hyperdrive 100k queries/day.
  - *Eligibility:* Anyone on a Free Cloudflare account.
  - *How to claim:* Create a free account, then deploy a Worker with `npx wrangler deploy`. No credit card needed.
  - *Expiry:* Daily limits reset each day; plan is perpetual.
  - *Gotchas:* The task's 'known so far' figure of ~1M requests/day is OUTDATED — the official current free limit is 100,000/day. Hitting the cap returns Error 1027 unless you upgrade to Workers Paid ($5/mo min).
  - *Source:* https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers/platform/limits/
- **Cloudflare Observability / Monitoring / Uptime free tier** (high) — Shared Observability free allowance of 0.5 GB/day ingestion + 7-day retention, pooling Traces, Workers Logs, Real User Monitoring (RUM), AI Gateway logs, R2 access logs, and Issues. Workers Logs free tier = 200,000 events/day (3-day retention). Cloudflare Radar and RUM are available on the Free plan for monitoring/uptime-style visibility.
  - *Eligibility:* Anyone on the Free plan; no CC or .edu required.
  - *How to claim:* In the dashboard, enable Analytics & Logs / Real User Monitoring; free to ingest up to 0.5 GB/day.
  - *Expiry:* No expiry; daily ingestion resets at 00:00 UTC.
  - *Gotchas:* When the 0.5 GB/day cap is hit, ingestion stops until the reset. Scheduled change: from Dec 1, 2026, Workers Logs moves to Cloudflare Observability pricing — plan retention/limits may change after that date.
  - *Source:* https://developers.cloudflare.com/observability/pricing/
- **Cloudflare Startup Program (up to $350,000 in credits)** (high) — 3 tiers (2026): Tier 3 = $10k (bootstrapped/self-funded, under $1M raised, no minimum funding); Tier 2 = $100k (under $5M raised, funded by an affiliated partner); Tier 1 = $350k ($5M+ raised, affiliated partner). Credits apply to usage-based services (Workers, KV, D1, Queues, Vectorize, R2 up to $10k, Workers AI up to $50k, Images, Stream, Argo, Cache Reserve). Included at NO COST on every tier: 3 enterprise domains, unlimited Business/Pro plans, DDoS + bot protection, WAF with 1,000 custom rules, CDN + DNS, load balancing, 100% SLA.
  - *Eligibility:* For-profit startup founded within the last 10 years, funded up to Series B within the last 12 months, actively building a tech product, with a public verifiable website, active on LinkedIn/X/GitHub, first-time applicant, and a business-domain email. Since Sep 2025, non-profit/civil-society/public-interest orgs are also eligible (up to $250k).
  - *How to claim:* Apply at cloudflare.com/startups (form describing the product + team). You must create a Cloudflare account AND add a valid payment method on file (used to bill overages). Review takes up to ~48 hours; approval arrives by email.
  - *Expiry:* Credits valid for 1 year or until fully consumed, whichever is first. No extensions; upgrading within a cohort does not reset the 12-month window.
  - *Gotchas:* Unlike the plain Free plan, the Startup program REQUIRES a payment method on file. Product-specific caps: R2 up to $10k; Workers AI $2.5k (Tier 3)/$10k (Tier 2)/$50k (Tier 1); AI Gateway temporarily NOT covered. 'Funded up to Series B within last 12 months' means recently-fundraised startups only.
  - *Source:* https://www.cloudflare.com/startups/
- **Cloudflare Academy — free courses & certification (education license)** (medium) — Self-paced, free online training curriculum plus free certification exams (e.g. Cloudflare Certified Associate). No .edu email, no student status, and no funding cap required — open to anyone. Educators can also run the material in classrooms; instructors can be supported through Cloudflare's instructor program.
  - *Eligibility:* Open to anyone (students, educators, self-learners, all regions).
  - *How to claim:* Go to Cloudflare Academy, create a free account, and start any course for $0.
  - *Expiry:* Courses/certs do not expire; content updates over time.
  - *Gotchas:* Could not re-fetch the Academy page today (DNS resolution failure for academy.cloudflare.com from this environment), so the exact current course catalog/URL was not re-verified. The free/open/self-paced model and free-certification positioning are consistent with Cloudflare's learning/community pages but were not re-read on the live page.
  - *Source:* https://academy.cloudflare.com/
- **Cloudflare Ambassador & Community programs (student leaders)** (high) — The Cloudflare Community Ambassador program explicitly targets 'student leaders' (alongside community organizers and technical creators): run on-campus clubs, meetups, workshops, and content. Benefits include Cloudflare credits, marketing assets, swag, and access to Cloudflare experts; cohorts last 1 year with the option to reapply. A separate Community Engineer track provides discretionary grants for meaningful open-source work. Neither is a paid role.
  - *Eligibility:* Open application; non-technical enthusiasts welcome; student club leaders, community organizers, and open-source contributors.
  - *How to claim:* Apply through cloudflare.com/community under the Ambassador (or Community Engineer) track.
  - *Expiry:* Cohort-based: 1-year Ambassador cohorts, reapply to continue; Community Engineer has no fixed term.
  - *Gotchas:* Credits/grants are discretionary (awards, not a flat entitlement) and cohort-based, not a guaranteed free tier like the Free plan.
  - *Source:* https://www.cloudflare.com/community/
- **Cloudflare Free (Network & CDN) plan — always-free** (high) — $0/month website/CDN plan: CDN, unmetered DDoS protection, Universal SSL, WAF with Free Managed Ruleset, DNS, SSO, Role-based Account Control; 70 WAF rules + 10K bulk redirects; bot mitigation limited to 'Simple bots'; community forums support only. Paid tiers for comparison: Pro $20/mo annual ($25 monthly), Business $200/mo annual ($250 monthly), Enterprise custom. Page targets 'personal or hobby projects'.
  - *Eligibility:* Open to anyone worldwide; no .edu email, no student status, no credit card/payment method required for the Free plan (sign up at dash.cloudflare.com/sign-up, 'Start building for free' — payment method only needed later to enable paid products/services).
  - *How to claim:* Create a free Cloudflare account (no payment method), add your domain as a zone, set nameservers to Cloudflare. Instant, perpetual, renews monthly at $0. The plan page (network-cdn) does not state a hard zone/site cap or bandwidth cap; Free has historically been unlimited-bandwidth with all sites/zones included.
  - *Expiry:* Permanent; no expiry. Free-plan feature sets can shrink/change with plan revisions.
  - *Gotchas:* Only 'Simple bots' mitigation and 70 rules on Free; WAF max payload and bot analytics are paid-plan features. Paid add-ons cost extra (e.g. Log Explorer $1/GB after 10 GB).
  - *Source:* https://www.cloudflare.com/plans/network-cdn/
- **Cloudflare Developer Free plan — Workers, R2, D1, KV, Pages, AI (perpetual, not a trial)** (high) — Perpetual free allowances (monthly unless noted): Workers 100,000 requests/day + 10 ms CPU/request (128 MB memory; 100 Workers; 5 cron triggers; 50 external subrequests/request); R2 10 GB-month Standard storage + 1M Class A + 10M Class B ops/month; D1 5 GB total storage + 5M rows read/day; Workers KV 1 GB storage + 100K reads/day; Pages 500 builds/month, 100 custom domains/project, 20K files, 20-min build timeout; Workers AI 10,000 neurons/day; Observability free: 0.5 GB/day ingestion, 7-day retention; Workflows 3,000 steps/day + 1 GB-month storage. No charge for egress/bandwidth on Workers and R2. Workers Paid plan is $5/mo minimum (includes 10M requests + 30M CPU ms).
  - *Eligibility:* Open to anyone; no .edu/student requirement, no funding/company status, and credit card NOT required to create a Free account — docs explicitly frame adding a payment method as a post-signup step to 'enable additional products and services'.
  - *How to claim:* Sign up free at dash.cloudflare.com/sign-up; free allowances are included automatically on any new account — no application, no key, no card. Exceeding Workers quotas returns error 1027 (fail-open/closed), not a bill, on the Free plan; overages on R2/D1/KV etc. do bill, which requires a payment method on the account.
  - *Expiry:* Permanent free tier; daily limits reset at midnight UTC, monthly allowances reset each billing month. 2026 additions started charging for some add-ons (see sunset_notes).
  - *Gotchas:* R2 free tier covers Standard storage only (Infrequent Access is never free). New metered add-ons (R2 SQL, R2 Data Catalog billed 2026-08-03; AI Search billing from 2026-11-01 with 5M ingestion tokens, 10 GB-month, 1K semantic + 1K full-text queries/month included) can produce invoices on an otherwise-free account — monitor 'Billable Usage' and set spend caps. Workers AI Gateway is 'temporarily not covered' by startup credits.
  - *Source:* https://www.cloudflare.com/plans/
- **Cloudflare Startup Program — annual credits ($10K / $100K / $350K tiers)** (high) — Cloudflare's startup program (cloudflare.com/startup, apply at /lp/startups) offers credits valid 1 year or until consumed (extensions not granted). Tiers recently raised: Tier 3 $10,000/yr (was $5,000), 'no minimum funding required'; Tier 2 $100,000/yr + account manager; Tier 1 $350,000/yr (was $250,000) + Office Hours + priority support. Exclusions/caps: R2 capped at $10K credit; Workers AI capped at $2.5K/$10K/$50K by tier; AI Gateway temporarily not covered; domain registrar fees and premium network services (Magic Transit, Enterprise Spectrum, Dedicated IPs, China Network) excluded. Review typically ~48 hours, confirmation by email.
  - *Eligibility:* For-profit company only; incorporated no more than 10 years ago; funded up to Series B (funding within last 12 months, except Tier 3 needs no minimum funding); business email on the company domain — personal emails are rejected, so .edu/student emails are NOT eligible; must have a public website and public LinkedIn/X/GitHub presence; first-time applicant only; no region limit is stated on the page.
  - *How to claim:* Apply via the form at https://www.cloudflare.com/lp/startups with company/business-domain email; if accepted you receive the tier's annual credit to offset Cloudflare spend. The startup program is for companies, so students/universities/sole .edu users effectively cannot claim it.
  - *Expiry:* Credits: 12 months from grant or until consumed, whichever first; no extensions. Active members near old caps may email startups@cloudflare.com for case-by-case tier upgrade (does not reset the 12-month window).
  - *Gotchas:* Business-entity + company-domain email rules make it unusable for individual students, classes, or nonprofit/university projects; 'funding within last 12 months' + up-to-Series-B cap means late-stage and >10-year-old companies are out; entry tier is now $10K not the $5K referenced in older sources.
  - *Source:* https://www.cloudflare.com/startup/
- **Education / .edu programs — no dedicated student or university program found** (medium) — No .edu-only or student-exclusive Cloudflare offer was found today: no Cloudflare education license, no student credits, no university pricing page (cloudflare.com/academy and /lp/startups-education equivalents 404; academy.cloudflare.com did not resolve in this session; Cloudflare docs contain no 'academy/education' content). Cloudflare Academy (free courses/certifications for IT/security training, open to everyone — not .edu-gated) has existed in recent years, but its current status is unconfirmed from today's fetches. The only 'free' education-adjacent value is the general free developer plan plus free Radar data dashboards.
  - *Eligibility:* n/a — negative finding: no .edu/student-email program exists to claim; free platform is available to students exactly as to anyone else (no card required).
  - *How to claim:* Nothing to claim. Students get the standard Free developer plan (Workers 100K req/day, R2 10 GB, D1 5 GB, KV 1 GB, Pages 500 builds/month) with no special student terms; universities would have to use the startup program only if they fit its for-profit company rules, which they typically do not.
  - *Expiry:* n/a
  - *Gotchas:* If Cloudflare Academy has moved hosts (dead subdomain in this environment), re-verify before citing its free courses/certifications; treat any .edu-only claim as unsubstantiated.
  - *Source:* https://academy.cloudflare.com/(DNSdidnotresolve,2026-10-22)
- **2025–26 change:** Changes in 2025–2026: (1) The Cloudflare for Startups program was restructured from the April 2025 four-tier layout ($5k/$25k/$100k/$250k, per blog post 'Startup Program update') to the current three-tier layout ($10k/$100k/$350k) — Tier 3 raised $5k→$10k and top tier $250k→$350k; regional pages (e.g. cloudflare.com/en-in/forstartups) still display the outdated $250k/4-tier structure, so use the US .com page as authoritative. (2) Sept 22, 2025 (Birthday Week 2025): Students program launched (12 months free Workers Paid for US .edu 18+) and the program was extended to non-profits/civil-society orgs (up to $250k credits). (3) Sept 2025: 'Come build with us' hubs — program participants get office access in SF/Austin/London/Lisbon from Jan 2026. (4) Sept 28, 2026: 'The Cold Start' competition announced — 5 early-stage companies pitch at Cloudflare Connect; grand prize $500,000 in credits (competition, not a standing program). (5) Workers AI pricing page (last updated Oct 20, 2026) moved to per-model granular unit-based pricing display while backend billing remains Neuron-based; 8 frontier models are now gated behind a paid billing method. Sunset of 2022–2024 programs: the original $1.25B/$2B-pledge Workers Launchpad funding program (2022) evolved into the current cohort Launchpad; no free-offer sunsets this cycle — all five offers above remain active as of 2026-10-22. Note: one follow-up web search (non-profit program page URL) could not be executed because the session's WebSearch budget was exhausted; non-profit and Launchpad findings lean on blog/press summaries and cached page content, hence medium confidence.
- **2025–26 change:** 2025–2026 program changes: (1) The Startup Program was restructured in 2025 from the April 2025 4-tier structure ($5,000 / $25,000 / $100,000 / $250,000, 'founded within last 5 years') to the current 3 tiers ($10k / $100k / $350k, founded within last 10 years) — entry tier doubled, top tier raised $250k->$350k, and the $25k tier was dropped. Members on the old caps can request a case-by-case upgrade that does not reset their 12-month expiry. (2) Sept 22, 2025: program expanded to non-profit, civil-society, and public-interest organizations (up to $250k credits; first application window ran until Dec 1, 2025). (3) Sept 22, 2025: Cloudflare for Students launched (12-month free paid developer features, US .edu only) — an education license/credit that did not exist before 2025. (4) 'The Cold Start' ($500k credit prize) closed with its Oct 19, 2026 Connect event; applications ended Oct 2, 2026. (5) Stale pages: some localized pages (e.g., cloudflare.com/en-in/forstartups/) still show the old '5 years / $5k-$250k' criteria — treat as outdated; the global /startups/ page is authoritative. (6) Community note: a global Cloudflare outage on Nov 18, 2025 (Workers KV, Access, Turnstile, dashboard, core CDN) is a recurring single-vendor risk with free tiers. The pre-launch claim 'WAF (1,000 rules) on all [startup] tiers' is confirmed — but the regular Free plan only gets 5 WAF custom rules; 1,000 is the Enterprise-plan limit that the startup program includes at no cost.
- **2025–26 change:** As of 2026-10-22, Cloudflare has NO distinct free offers targeted at students or startups: (1) No startup program — the official partners page (cloudflare.com/partners, fetched 2026-10-22) lists only PowerUP channel program, technology alliances, global SIs, service providers, and the Cloudflare@OCI alliance; no startup track, no free credits, no age/funding-capped program. (2) No trial credits — paid Workers plans simply start at $5/mo with usage-based overage; no free-trial-of-paid credits are offered. (3) No education license/.edu discount for infrastructure — cloudflare.com/plans (fetched 2026-10-22) shows usage-based Free/Paid tiers only and mentions no education, student, or nonprofit discount; no .edu email is required for any tier; the free tiers are universally available to any account.
