### Notion for Education

- **Notion Free Plan (individual)** (high) — $0/yr. For a single-owner (1-person) workspace: unlimited pages & blocks. A free workspace with 2+ owners is capped at 1,000 blocks per workspace (3-day grace before enforcement). File uploads up to 5 MB each. Page/edit history 7 days. Up to 10 external guests on a single-member workspace. AI features (Notion Agent, Meeting Notes, Research Mode) are available only as a 'limited trial' with no published fixed free-credit allotment; extra Notion credits cost $10/1,000 (monthly) or $13/1,000 (annual), and Standard/Select AI credits are bundled only on Business/Enterprise.
  - *Eligibility:* Open to anyone; no .edu email required, no credit card to sign up, no stated region limits.
  - *How to claim:* Create a Notion account (web/desktop); you land on the Free plan by default. No card required.
  - *Expiry:* No expiry; the free tier is permanent. The AI 'limited trial' is not a recurring free entitlement and continued heavy AI use requires purchasing credits.
  - *Gotchas:* Trial-vs-free: AI on the Free plan is a limited trial, not a fixed free-credit pool — heavy AI use pushes you into $10/1,000 credits. The 1,000-block cap only triggers with 2+ workspace owners. Notion does not publish a total-storage figure; the cap is 5 MB per file.
  - *Source:* https://www.notion.com/pricing
- **Notion for Education — free individual student/teacher plan** (high) — Free Education plan for a 1-member workspace (individual student or teacher): unlimited pages & blocks, up to 100 guests per workspace, unlimited uploads, 30-day edit history. No AI-credit amount is published. Genuinely free, not a trial.
  - *Eligibility:* Students/teachers at accredited colleges or universities listed in the World Higher Education Database (WHED). K-12 students and K-12 educators are NOT eligible. You must sign in with your institutional (.edu/college) email as the account's MAIN email (a secondary school email does not count). Notion does not accept student IDs or other documentation. Each school email claims it once, for one workspace.
  - *How to claim:* Be on the Free plan with a single member. Settings -> 'Upgrade plan' -> 'Get free education plan'. If your school is in WHED, the upgrade is automatic once the institutional email is verified.
  - *Expiry:* No set end date. Re-verify your school email annually (unlimited re-verifications). Losing your valid school email downgrades the workspace (and all its content) to the Free plan. Upgrading to Plus/Business/Enterprise permanently ends Education eligibility for that workspace.
  - *Gotchas:* Upgrading to a paid plan is irreversible for Education eligibility on that workspace. Notion/AI credit counts and per-file size caps are not stated on the page. The individual claim is desktop/web only, not mobile.
  - *Source:* https://www.notion.com/help/notion-for-education
- **Notion for Education — free student-organization plan (unlimited members)** (high) — Free plan for a student-organization workspace with unlimited members: unlimited blocks/files, up to 100 guests, unlimited uploads, 30-day edit history.
  - *Eligibility:* Student organizations at accredited colleges/universities in WHED; one-time application (one application per organization). Same institutional-email verification as the individual plan.
  - *How to claim:* Apply via the application link on the education help page; on approval the org workspace is granted. One application per organization.
  - *Expiry:* No set end date; annual re-verification; loss of a valid school email reverts the workspace to the Free plan.
  - *Gotchas:* One application/approval per organization; not stackable across multiple orgs.
  - *Source:* https://www.notion.com/help/notion-for-education
- **GitHub Student Developer Pack — Notion perk** (medium) — Verified students receive 'everything in the Notion Education plan with additional AI responses' (i.e., the free Education-plan entitlements above plus extra AI responses; the exact AI quantity is not published). A separate 'Notion Template Collection' (course dashboards, hackathon tools, portfolio templates) is also in the pack.
  - *Eligibility:* Requires GitHub student verification (.edu email + verified student status via the GitHub Student Pack). No credit card required to claim the pack.
  - *How to claim:* Verify student status on the GitHub Student Developer Pack, then claim the Notion perk; it links into Notion's Education-plan flow.
  - *Expiry:* Tied to GitHub student-verification validity; the underlying Notion education plan persists per its own annual re-verification.
  - *Gotchas:* The perk was historically 'free Notion Pro (annual)' for verified students; it is now reworded to the Notion Education plan + additional AI responses — the underlying plan type changed. 'Additional AI responses' quantity is unspecified.
  - *Source:* https://education.github.com/pack
- **Notion for Startups — up to 6 months free Business plan (with Notion AI)** (high) — Eligible companies get free months on the Business plan (which bundles Notion AI). Tiers: 6 months for partner-affiliated startups (via AWS Activate, Stripe Atlas, HubSpot, Shopify, JP Morgan, etc.); 3 months for non-partner startups meeting criteria; 1 month for SMBs/small teams (<10 employees) or when verification fails. The 6-month track needs a valid Notion domain, a public company website, a company email, and <100 employees. Must be a new, non-paying Notion customer.
  - *Eligibility:* Startups building scalable tech-related products/services; partner route or non-partner route. No stated funding cap or minimum company age; headcount limits apply (6-mo: <100; 1-mo: <10). No region limits stated. No credit card requirement per the doc.
  - *How to claim:* Partner route: get your partner's unique code, then submit the form at notion.so/startups-apply. Non-partner route: fill the same application form. Notion emails to confirm; the upgrade takes effect immediately. 3-5 business-day review. One redemption; multiple partners do not stack.
  - *Expiry:* The free window is fixed (1/3/6 months), after which the Business plan continues as a paid subscription unless downgraded.
  - *Gotchas:* Trial-vs-free: after the free months you are on a paid Business plan — downgrade to avoid charges. Applies only to net-new Business plans (not Enterprise); no refunds for previously paid invoices. A failed/weak verification (inactive site, solo applicant, limited footprint) can downgrade a 3/6-month request to 1 month.
  - *Source:* https://www.notion.com/help/notion-for-startups
- **Notion Business Plan 30-day trial (no credit card)** (high) — 30-day trial of the full Business Plan (includes Notion AI) at $0. No credit card to start; payment is collected only if you upgrade. Auto-renews at trial end if you added payment details; otherwise you are prompted to confirm the upgrade.
  - *Eligibility:* Requires a professional domain; educational and personal domains are excluded. Web/desktop only. No credit card required to begin.
  - *How to claim:* Start the Business Plan trial from Notion's settings/billing on a workspace using a professional domain.
  - *Expiry:* 30 days, then auto-renew (if a card was added) or a prompt to confirm; canceling drops you to the Free plan (or back to Plus if the trial started on Plus).
  - *Gotchas:* Trial, not a free tier: if you add a card it auto-renews into a paid Business plan. .edu / personal / free email-provider domains are excluded. This is a gateway to a paid plan, distinct from the genuinely-free tiers above.
  - *Source:* https://www.notion.com/help/paid-plan-trials
- **2025–26 change:** Changes 2025-2026: (1) K-12 students and K-12 educators are NOT eligible for Notion's free Education plan — only students/teachers at accredited higher-ed institutions in the World Higher Education Database; K-12 users fall back to the general Free plan. This narrows the historical "free for all educators" posture. (2) The GitHub Student Developer Pack Notion perk, historically "free Notion Pro (annual)", is now listed as "everything in the Notion Education plan with additional AI responses" — the underlying plan shifted from Pro to Education, and extra AI is now metered. (3) AI is now usage-metered via "Notion credits" ($10/1,000 monthly; $13/1,000 annual); Standard/Select AI credits are bundled only on Business/Enterprise, and the Free/Education plans list AI as a limited trial with no published fixed free-credit amount. No core free offer was fully sunset in 2025-2026 — the free Education plan (higher-ed), the free student-org plan, the individual Free plan, the Startup program, and the 30-day Business trial all remain active as of 2026-10-22. Method note: web search budget was exhausted this session, so all findings above were verified by WebFetch of official pages (notion.com pricing, education/startups/trials/block-usage/credits help articles, education.github.com/pack) today; not a search-based synthesis.

### Oracle Cloud Infrastructure Always Free

- **OCI Always Free services (Free Tier — no expiry)** (high) — Always Free limits (oracle.com Free Tier service list, re-fetched live 2026-10-22): Compute = 2 AMD VMs (each 1/8 OCPU + 1 GB RAM) and ARM = 1,500 OCPU-hours + 9,000 GB-hours/mo (Ampere A1, ~2 OCPU / 12 GB RAM, usable as 1 or 2 VMs). Storage = 200 GB block (2 block volumes + 5 backups) + 20 GB object (standard+infrequent+archive combined) + 50,000 object API requests/mo. Networking = 10 TB/mo outbound egress, 2 VCNs, 1 Load Balancer (10 Mbps) + 1 Flexible NLB, 50 site-to-site VPN connections. Databases = up to 2 Autonomous DBs total (ATP/ADW/AJD/APEX), NoSQL (133M reads + 133M writes/mo, 25 GB/table up to 3 tables), 1 HeatWave (50 GB storage + 50 GB backup). Plus APEX 744 hrs/instance, Email 100/day, 5 Bastions, monitoring/logging within free quotas. Always Free services are usable for an unlimited period.
  - *Eligibility:* Open to any individual/account — no .edu or student-email requirement, no startup requirement. One Free Trial or Always Free account per person. Available in regions where commercial OCI is offered; some services (e.g., ARM, Autonomous DB) only in select/home regions.
  - *How to claim:* Self-serve sign-up at oracle.com/cloud/free/ -> create a tenancy in a supported region -> provision Always Free shapes/services within the limits above. No charge while you stay within Always Free amounts. A credit/credit-like debit card is required at sign-up.
  - *Expiry:* No expiry / no renewal — Always Free has no time limit. Caveat: accounts idle for 30+ days may be deemed abandoned and suspended/terminated.
  - *Gotchas:* Card IS collected at sign-up for identity verification (a ~$1 temporary authorization hold is removed in 3-5 days; PIN, virtual, single-use and prepaid cards are NOT accepted). Do not conflate Always Free with the 30-day $300 trial. ARM is ~2 OCPU/12 GB (up to 2 VMs), NOT '4 micro VMs'; the 200 GB is the block-storage pool, not a per-VM figure. Autonomous DB capped at 2 instances; per-DB storage and region availability ('select regions') are not stated on the free page. Egress beyond 10 TB/mo and storage beyond free quotas are billed.
  - *Source:* https://www.oracle.com/cloud/free/(per-servicelimitsinhttps://www.oracle.com/a/ocom/docs/oci-free-tier_v1.json)
- **30-day Free Trial with US$300 credit (all OCI services)** (high) — Sign-up grants US$300 of credit usable on all eligible OCI services for 30 days, on top of Always Free. During the 30-day window you can also use the larger 'Trial' allowances listed on the page: Compute up to 4,500 hrs + 500 GB storage; Storage up to 7 TB block + 11 TB object; Autonomous DB up to 3,338 hrs + 2 TB Exadata; plus trial quotas for Functions, Kubernetes, WebLogic, HeatWave (5,000 hrs), Load Balancer, etc.
  - *Eligibility:* Any individual/account (students and startups alike) — no .edu or startup criteria. One Free Trial per person; a valid credit/credit-like debit card is required at sign-up for identity verification.
  - *How to claim:* Same sign-up as the Free Tier (oracle.com/cloud/free/); the $300 trial credit attaches automatically to the new account. Spend it within 30 days. Optionally convert to Pay-As-You-Go at any point by adding a payment method.
  - *Expiry:* US$300 OR 30 days, whichever comes first; then the account drops to Always Free only (or Pay-As-You-Go if converted). Idle 30+ days may trigger suspension.
  - *Gotchas:* Card is REQUIRED at sign-up (temporary ~$1 auth hold; no PIN/virtual/prepaid cards). The $300 covers only the 30-day window — afterwards only Always Free remains unless you convert to Pay-As-You-Go. You are billed for data egress beyond 10 TB/mo, storage beyond free quotas, and any service not in Always Free. The live page shows US$300 (older marketing referenced a smaller figure; confirm $300, not $200).
  - *Source:* https://www.oracle.com/cloud/free/
- **Oracle Startup program (application-based startup credit)** (low) ⚠ low confidence: verify before claiming — A separate, application-based credit program for startups, layered on top of the self-serve $300 trial. I could NOT read the live program page today: the legacy URL oracle.com/startupcloud/ returns 404 and oracle.com/startups/ is bot-gated (WAF 403) from this environment, so current credit amounts, duration, funding-cap and company-age criteria could not be independently confirmed.
  - *Eligibility:* Startups (application-based; typically partner/ecosystem criteria). Exact company-age / funding-cap limits not verifiable today.
  - *How to claim:* Apply via the Oracle startup program portal (oracle.com/startups/ or a successor URL); on approval the startup receives cloud credit. Exact 2026 steps and limits unverified.
  - *Expiry:* Unknown — could not verify the live program terms today.
  - *Gotchas:* Do not rely on third-party numbers: the official page was inaccessible today (404/403), so re-verify amounts, funding cap, and duration in a normal browser. Distinguish this application-based program from the universal $300 trial / Always Free tier, which anyone can use.
  - *Source:* https://www.oracle.com/startups/(legacyhttps://www.oracle.com/startupcloud/—bothinaccessible2026-10-22)
- **Education front — Oracle University (training) and Oracle Cloud Academy (appears sunset); no .edu-only free cloud offer** (medium) — No distinct .edu-gated or student-email-only free cloud-credit program was found. The 'education' front is Oracle University (education.oracle.com), a free training/certification/skills-development portal (it confers no OCI cloud credit). The former 'Oracle Cloud Academy' (education.oracle.com/cloud-academy), which historically provided free OCI credits to learners/instructors, now returns a '404 / Oracle University' error page as of today, indicating that offering has been sunset or redirected.
  - *Eligibility:* No .edu requirement for any current Oracle free cloud offer. Oracle University is open (no student-email gate).
  - *How to claim:* Students obtain cloud for free only via the universal Free Tier + $300 trial (a card is required). Oracle University courses are free at education.oracle.com but grant no OCI credit.
  - *Expiry:* Cloud Academy appears ended (its URL serves a 404/error page today).
  - *Gotchas:* Do not expect a student/.edu discount or dedicated student credit — use the standard sign-up. Some Oracle University certifications have paid components even though basic courses are free. Verify Cloud Academy status separately; as of 2026-10-22 its URL is an error page.
  - *Source:* https://education.oracle.com/;https://education.oracle.com/cloud-academy(404today)
- **2025–26 change:** Material 2025-2026 changes observed: (1) Oracle Cloud Academy (education.oracle.com/cloud-academy) — the last education-only free-credits front — now serves a '404 / Oracle University' error page as of 2026-10-22, i.e. it appears sunset; the education front is now just Oracle University (free training, no cloud credit). (2) Legacy startup program URL oracle.com/startupcloud/ returns 404; its successor oracle.com/startups/ is WAF/bot-gated (403) so live terms were unreadable today — treat startup-program figures as unverified. (3) The trial landing URL oracle.com/cloud/free/trial/ now 404s; the trial is folded into /cloud/free/. (4) Trial credit is US$300 for 30 days on the live page (older docs referenced a smaller credit). (5) ARM Always Free is ~2 OCPU/12 GB (up to 2 VMs), not '4 micro VMs' as in the task's known note (the 200 GB figure is the block-storage pool). Note: the oracle.com/cloud/free/ page carries a metadata 'Updated Date' of 2024-09-10, so the Always Free limits have not been re-edited since that date per the page metadata; values were re-fetched live on 2026-10-22. WebSearch was unavailable this session (budget exhausted), so all verifications were done by directly fetching official Oracle pages; the /startups/ and /startupcloud/ pages could not be read.
- **Critic correction** (4 ARM micro VMs (200 GB storage), 10 TB egress ...): The Always Free Ampere A1 allowance is one flexible pool of 1,500 OCPU-hours and 9,000 GB-hours per month, equal to 2 OCPUs and 12 GB RAM. It is not four ARM micro VMs. Always Free also includes up to two AMD VM.Standard.E2.1.Micro instances and 200 GB of block storage.

### Unity Personal

- **Unity Personal (free tier)** (high) — Perpetually free editor + cloud for hobbyists and small indie teams. No credit card required (stated on the pricing page). Includes: publish to web/desktop/AR/VR/mobile, Unity Cloud ecosystem, MCP access, 1,000 AI tool credits/month. As of the 3/1/2026 DevOps change the free cloud tier includes: unlimited public-cloud Version Control seats, 25 GB standard storage per org/month, 10 GB Asset Manager storage, 100 GB free egress, 2 concurrent build units, and 200 Windows (Micro) + 100 Linux (Micro) + 100 Mac (Standard) free build minutes/month. Made with Unity splash screen optional on Unity 6. No Runtime Fee (canceled Sep 2024). Overages: $0.75/GB Asset Manager, $0.10/GB standard storage >25GB, $0.10/GB egress >100GB, Micro $0.02/min, Standard Linux $0.06 & Mac/Windows $0.07, Premium $0.11-0.12, Ultra $0.18-0.20, Turbo Windows $0.37; extra editor concurrency $10/mo.
  - *Eligibility:* Individuals/hobbyists/small businesses whose total finances (revenue + funding, trailing 12 months) are under $200,000 USD; if serving third-party clients, the aggregate client revenue/funding must be under $200K. Gaming/entertainment apps only — non-gaming apps where total company finances exceed $1,000,000 require Unity Industry, and Industry customers may not use Personal. No age floor, no credit card, self-reported finances subject to audit. Region: global; page/ToS in USD.
  - *How to claim:* Create a free Unity ID, download Unity Hub and Editor from unity.com/download, select the Personal plan. Self-certify finances in the Unity dashboard. No card required. Console (PS/Xbox/Switch) and Apple Vision Pro builds need Pro instead.
  - *Expiry:* No expiry while under the $200K threshold; must upgrade to Pro once exceeded (immediately upon crossing; tiers cannot be mixed).
  - *Gotchas:* Exceeding $200K forces paid Pro ($2,310/yr or $210/mo post-Jan 2026); no downgrades during a commitment window; cloud storage/egress/build-minute overages bill even on the free plan; splash screen is optional only on Unity 6; industry (non-game) use above $1M total finances is ineligible.
  - *Source:* https://unity.com/pricing(alsohttps://unity.com/products/unity-personal)
- **Unity Student plan (free education license)** (high) — Free plan giving students the full Unity Pro-tier editor (no education watermark) plus: free Odin Inspector + Validator education licenses, a premium Synty asset bundle, Unity Cloud personal tier (3 Version Control seats, 5 GB storage), and a 20% Asset Store discount. Valid 1 year; renews while enrolled (renew any time up to 60 days before expiry or after it expires). A school email is NOT required — 'You don't need a school email to sign up'; verification is via SheerID (or SAML sign-in to school where available). K-12 tier explicitly 16+. One Unity ID per verified account; student projects are isolated to the Student-plan account.
  - *Eligibility:* Students of legal age to consent to personal data processing (age 13 in the U.S., 16 in the E.U.; secondary tier stated as 16+), currently enrolled in credit-bearing classes at an accredited institution. No .edu email required. Global (page published in 9 languages); SheerID does regional verification.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Apply at unity.com/products/unity-student, choose Post-Secondary or Secondary. 2) Create/log in to Unity ID. 3) Complete the SheerID form with academic details + documentation (instant most of the time; up to ~5 business days). 4) Redeem via the emailed instructions: download Unity Hub, then activate the license key (Unity Hub > Licenses > Activate with serial number).
  - *Expiry:* 1 year; renewable indefinitely while actively enrolled, re-verified through SheerID. Graduating = plan lapses after its year (no special post-graduation path stated).
  - *Gotchas:* Cannot be shared; friends/family must apply separately; may log in at school but it's a personal (not lab/classroom) license — institutions use the Education Grant License; projects under the Student plan can't be opened in Personal/Educator/EGL accounts; commercializing student projects is allowed under the Editor ToS; legacy GitHub Student Pack references in some university IT articles are outdated (SheerID is current).
  - *Source:* https://unity.com/products/unity-student
- **Unity Educator plan (free education license)** (high) — Free individual educator plan (cannot be purchased): Unity Pro-tier editor without watermark, free Odin Inspector + Validator education licenses, Synty asset bundle, an 'educator asset pack' (industry-built modular assets for demos/sample projects), 20% Asset Store discount, and Version Control with 3 seats / 5 GB. For creating/teaching/sharing Unity projects at home, school, or elsewhere — not for deploying across institutional machines.
  - *Eligibility:* Higher education: educators at accredited post-secondary degree-granting institutions globally (SheerID affiliation verification; .edu email not strictly required). K-12: available in roughly 30 listed countries (US, CA, AU, UK, etc.); K-12 educators in unlisted regions must use the Education Grant License instead. One Unity ID per verified account.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Apply at unity.com/products/unity-educator, choose K-12 or Higher Education. 2) Create/log in to Unity ID. 3) Complete SheerID form, upload affiliation proof if asked. 4) License key arrives by email; activate in Unity Hub > Licenses > Activate with serial number.
  - *Expiry:* 1 year; renew by returning to the page and re-verifying with SheerID (no purchase path exists).
  - *Gotchas:* Not for multi-computer classroom/lab deployment (use Education Grant License); K-12 is region-gated; educator projects can't be accessed from Student/EGL accounts; individual-use only.
  - *Source:* https://unity.com/products/unity-educator
- **Unity Education Grant License (institutional / university program)** (high) — Free multi-seat license that institutions deploy across lab/classroom computers and servers (no Unity ID sign-in required on the machines). Covers the core Unity engine (build/optimize/deploy across 25+ platforms) 'regardless of classroom size'. A full year of licenses at no cost, renewable annually at no cost; seat counts modifiable after initial approval; one Unity ID tied to the verified institutional account; no Unity Cloud access; EGL projects cannot be accessed via Student/Educator accounts. Accredited institutions: free. Nonprofits: available at a discounted (paid) cost instead. Students can pair EGL on lab machines with the free Student plan on personal devices.
  - *Eligibility:* Academic institutions accredited by an authorized governmental agency (local/state/provincial/federal/national) whose primary purpose is teaching enrolled students; .edu/institutional email used for the application. Outside-U.S. K-12 educators whose region lacks the K-12 Educator plan may use EGL. No age floor (institutional).
  - *How to claim:* 1) Sign in at unity.com/products/unity-education-grant-license. 2) Select institution type. 3) Complete the SheerID verification form (up to ~48 h). 4) Submit the license request; follow the Unity Learn activation tutorial. Renew via the Unity Education License Information dashboard > Renew License, entering seat count.
  - *Expiry:* 1-year term, renewable; renewal window opens 90 days before expiry; annual re-grant.
  - *Gotchas:* Changing the associated institutional email requires a support ticket; the page still notes Unity Cloud is not part of EGL; research/industry/commercial projects at an institution should instead buy discounted Unity Pro through an education rep.
  - *Source:* https://unity.com/products/unity-education-grant-license
- **Unity AI tools free trial + plan AI credit allowance** (high) — Unity AI (MCP/AI tools) offers a 14-day no-commitment free trial (link from the products page into the Unity service store, variant 'uty-pts-no-commitment-14-day-trial'); after the trial a paid monthly points subscription. Separately, every Unity plan includes a monthly AI-credit allowance: Unity Personal 1,000 credits/month, Pro 2,000, Enterprise/Industry 3,000, with 'special terms apply to Unity Credits'.
  - *Eligibility:* Any Unity ID holder can take the 14-day AI trial; the free monthly allowance attaches to a Personal (or higher) plan. No credit card stated for the trial variant ('no-commitment').
  - *How to claim:* From unity.com/products, click 'Try Unity's AI tools free' (14-day variant). Or just use the plan's included monthly AI credits (Personal = 1,000/month).
  - *Expiry:* Trial: 14 days, then paid. Free allowance: renews monthly with the plan while eligible.
  - *Gotchas:* Trial is trial, not a perpetual free tier; post-trial requires paying subscription; credit special terms apply; AI feature is newer so limits may shift.
  - *Source:* https://unity.com/products(trial:service-store.unity.com14-dayno-commitmentvariant)
- **Unity DevOps free-tier expansion (Mar 1, 2026) — free cloud build/VC layer on Personal** (high) — Effective 3/1/2026 Unity expanded the free (pay-as-you-go) cloud tier: seat charges for public-cloud Unity Version Control removed (the old 3-free-seat cap for Personal becomes unlimited; 1:1 seat limits dropped on paid plans), free storage 5 GB -> 25 GB per org per month, 100 GB free egress added (no egress billing at all in March-April 2026), free build minutes now 200 Windows (Micro) + 100 Mac (Standard) + 100 Linux (Micro) monthly, and Unity Editor Concurrency doubled from 1 to 2 (extra = $10/mo). This applies to everyone on a Unity ID, so students and sub-$200K startup teams get it free.
  - *Eligibility:* All Unity plans; effectively free for Personal users (students, hobbyists, startups under $200K revenue/funding).
  - *How to claim:* Automatic — included with a Unity Personal (or any) account; manage usage in the Unity Dashboard (Administration > Costs and usage reporting).
  - *Expiry:* Ongoing as of Oct 2026; announced 2025-11-10, effective 3/1/2026.
  - *Gotchas:* Beyond free limits, usage bills: storage >25GB $0.10/GB (premium $0.30/GB), egress >100GB $0.10/GB, Micro $0.02/min, Standard $0.06-0.07/min, Premium $0.11-0.12/min, Ultra $0.18-0.20/min; no downgrade during a commitment window; 'later in 2026' Unity DevOps gains were promised but may still be rolling out.
  - *Source:* https://unity.com/products/pricing-updates
- **Unity Multiplay Hosting $800 startup sign-up credit (service in transition)** (medium) — New Unity Multiplay Game Server Hosting organizations received $800 of sign-up credit, valid 6 months from sign-up, automatically applied across the whole organization (shared by all projects) until exhausted; consumed on subsequent invoices; dashboard shows real-time consumption. Caveat found today: Unity is winding down direct support — support runs through 31 March 2026, the software is licensed to Rocket Science Group, and the official docs page now issues a 308 redirect to rocketscience.gg/services/hosting, so new credits likely come via Rocket Science Group rather than Unity.
  - *Eligibility:* New Unity Multiplay Hosting users (i.e., game startups adding server hosting); no CC-free claim — standard Unity account.
  - *How to claim:* Historically: subscribe to Unity Gaming Services/Multiplay Hosting; the $800 credit applied automatically org-wide; track in Dashboard > Administration > Costs and usage reporting.
  - *Expiry:* Credit: 6 months from sign-up. Service: Unity direct support ends 31 March 2026; continuity via Rocket Science Group.
  - *Gotchas:* Trial-style credit, not a free tier; once credit expires, pay-per-usage applies; migration off Unity's direct service may be required after the support cutoff.
  - *Source:* https://docs.unity.com/multiplay-hosting/concepts/pricing-free-credit(nowredirectstohttps://www.rocketscience.gg/services/hosting)
- **Unity for Humanity grants (social-impact creator grants)** (medium) — Grant program providing financial support + mentorship + audience amplification to creators using real-time 3D for social impact (environment, health, education, accessibility). 2026 winners already announced (e.g., Amaru, I NEED SPACE, Project Ember); also runs the UNEP/Project Drawdown Environment & Sustainability Grant and Imagine Grant (with Common, 2021). Not a license or startup-specific program, but a real free-money front that students/indies run into.
  - *Eligibility:* Creators/changemakers (any region) applying through periodic grant calls; application-based and selective; open on unity.com/humanity.
  - *How to claim:* Apply via the Unity for Humanity grant application on unity.com/humanity when a cycle is open; winners and honorees are announced per year.
  - *Expiry:* Recurring annual/periodic grant cycles (2026 cycle ran; next cycle timing follows Unity's announcement).
  - *Gotchas:* Grants, not subscriptions — no guaranteed amount, selective; distinct from Unity's education/startup licensing.
  - *Source:* https://unity.com/humanity
- **2025–26 change:** As of 2026-10-22, no dedicated 'Unity Startup Program' exists on unity.com (no link in the products navigation; unity.com/startups and similar paths 404). Startups are covered by the free Unity Personal tier (<$200K revenue+funding/12 months) plus the expanded free DevOps cloud tier; paid Pro is $2,310/yr or $210/mo. Material 2024-2026 changes: (1) Sep 12, 2024: Unity canceled the controversial Runtime Fee for games; Personal's cap doubled from $100K to $200K USD at the same time, and the 'Made with Unity' splash screen became optional starting with Unity 6. (2) Jan 1, 2025: plan restructure — Unity Plus was absorbed/renamed to Unity Pro; Pro became mandatory above $200K and Enterprise above $25M total finances; Pro was $2,200/yr at that date. (3) Jan 12, 2026: 5% Pro/Enterprise price increase (Pro -> $2,310/yr, $210/mo); Havok Physics for Unity dropped from Pro/Enterprise/Industry on 6.0 LTS (and from Unity 6.3). (4) Mar 1, 2026: free DevOps cloud tier expansion (unlimited VCS seats, 25GB storage, 100GB egress, added Mac/Linux build minutes, 2 editor concurrency) — all free tiers above reflect this. (5) 31 Mar 2026: Unity Multiplay Game Server Hosting direct support ends; service licensed to Rocket Science Group, docs 308-redirect there, so its $800 startup credit is in transition. (6) Aug-Sep 2026: Unity Economy (Gaming Services) became free to use starting 25 Aug 2026 and stopped accepting new project signups 8 Sep 2026 — a de-facto free tier for existing configs but the product is sunsetting (no new signups; IAP/Live Content positioned as the successors). (7) Verification path: the legacy 'GitHub Student Developer Pack' route for Unity Student has been replaced by SheerID (plus optional SAML sign-in to school); .edu email is explicitly not required by Unity's current Student plan page. Older third-party university IT articles still cite the GitHub Pack requirement — outdated. No announced sunset of the Personal, Student, Educator, or Education Grant License offerings as of Oct 22, 2026.

### Zoho Catalyst & Zoho Classes

- **Catalyst Student Program: $250 usage / 6 months, no credit card, non-commercial only** (high) — Catalyst is free for students building non-commercial projects: full-stack hosting (frontends Next.js/React/Vue/Nuxt/Angular, serverless functions, managed data, built-in auth, AI/ML tooling). Each student project gets up to $250 of usage at no cost over a six-month period. Bandwidth is free, unlimited; unlimited collaborators (no per-seat pricing). If a project hits the $250 threshold, the application is temporarily stopped and students are NOT auto-charged; they can email Catalyst Support with project details to request an extension, and 'eligible projects can receive additional free usage' (repeatable, case-by-case review). No subscription fee, no credit card, no edu-email verification described on the official page; no region restriction mentioned. Program launched with the first cohort of 'Catalyst Student Clubs' at three Chennai, India colleges (RMKEC, RMKCET, RMDEC).
  - *Eligibility:* Students, legitimate non-commercial use only (learning, coursework, hackathon builds, side projects, experimentation). .edu email NOT required per official page; no region limit stated; credit card NOT required. Commercial use excluded.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Open https://catalyst.zoho.com/students/ and click 'Claim credits' (deep-links to standard catalyst.zoho.com signup). 2) Sign up with an email address or existing Zoho account (no card). 3) Build/deploy; usage counted up to $250 per 6-month window. 4) At the cap: contact Catalyst Support to request extended free usage; they review the project's student-program qualification.
  - *Expiry:* No sunset date published. The $250 allowance is measured over six months per project, after which the app is stopped (not charged) until an extension is approved by Support.
  - *Gotchas:* Non-commercial only — commercial projects must use the standard Catalyst account. The official claim flow just opens the standard signup, so activation of the no-card student allowance should be confirmed with Catalyst Support (support@zohocatalyst.com). Students hitting the cap lose their running app until approval. Note the distinction: this no-CC student allowance is separate from the generic $250 wallet credit for all new users, which DOES require adding a card. Program is new (announced Sept 2, 2026; blog Sept 6, 2026).
  - *Source:* https://catalyst.zoho.com/students/
- **Catalyst Free Tier: perpetual monthly free usage (all users, pay-as-you-go)** (high) — Monthly free usage that auto-renews, 'Forever, no expiry', across all components. Monthly free limits (assume single-component use; limits are account-wide across all orgs/projects): Functions 25,000 GB-seconds; AppSail 900 GB-minutes; Circuits 2,000 state transitions; Data Store 2 GB-days storage + 10,000 SELECT / 5,000 INSERT / 1,000 UPDATE / 1,000 DELETE requests, 10,000 bulk-write rows; Stratus 5 GB file storage, 10,000 downloads; NoSQL 1 GB table + 1 GB index storage; Cache 1,000 GET / 5,000 PUT; API Gateway 100,000 requests; Web Client Hosting 300,000 requests; Mail 100 emails; Push Notifications 500; Search 1,000 queries; Slate (frontend) 12 GB-minutes build & deploy (2vCPU 4GB), 500 MB CDN storage, 25,000 GB-seconds invocations; QuickML 1,000,000 LLM input / 500,000 output tokens, 1 GB data storage; Zia Services 100 calls per API; SmartBrowz 7,200 GB-seconds headless, 50 PDF/screenshot conversions; DevOps CI/CD 1,200 GB-minutes, 100 alerts. You get usage alerts at 90% of any component's limit.
  - *Eligibility:* All new Catalyst users on the pay-as-you-go pricing model. No credit card to sign up ('you don't need to provide your card details to sign up to Catalyst's free tier'); a payment method is requested only when a resource's free allowance is about to run out.
  - *How to claim:* Sign up at catalyst.zoho.com (email address, or existing Zoho account). Choose the pay-as-you-go model; free tier applies automatically, refreshed after each bill/payment cycle. Keep usage within the hard limits to stay free indefinitely; delete unused projects to avoid the minimum bill.
  - *Expiry:* None — 'Renewal: Monthly; Forever, no expiry'. Limits refresh monthly.
  - *Gotchas:* Hard limits are consolidated across your ENTIRE account (all orgs and projects), not per project. Exceeding any component's limit triggers pay-as-you-go billing for that resource, PLUS a minimum billing of $5/project/month (introduced 2024-04-01) — delete unused projects to avoid it. Listed limits assume a component is used alone; mixing components dilutes each allowance. The generic $250 new-customer credits sit on top of (are exclusive of) this free tier and require a card. Bandwidth/egress is free (Catalyst's stated position).
  - *Source:* https://catalyst.zoho.com/free-tier/
- **Catalyst Free Trial + one-time $250 production wallet credits (all new customers; card required for the credits)** (high) — Free Trial: free full-featured development environment (Functions, AppSail, QuickML, ConvoKraft, SmartBrowz, AutoML, auth, Web Client Hosting, APM, push/mail, etc.) that does NOT expire; up to 50 projects per account; no auto-charging unless you move a project to production; one account per new customer; signup needs only an email address. The $250 wallet credits apply to production usage when your first project is deployed live; they are a one-time benefit, valid 6 months from the date claimed, and are exclusive of the monthly free-tier allowance and of the $5/month free tier bundled with subscription plans. After credits + free tier are exhausted, pay-as-you-go billing applies.
  - *Eligibility:* All new Catalyst customers who have never had a Catalyst account or received wallet credits (students, startups, individuals — no commercial restrictions on this general path). Free Trial needs no card; the $250 production credits require adding a payment method ('Add a card to claim them').
  - *How to claim:* 1) Sign up at catalyst.zoho.com (email or Zoho account) — development use is free and non-expiring. 2) To claim $250: in Billing, click 'Get $250 Credits' → Select Payment Plan → add a payment method; credits become valid for 6 months. 3) Deploy first project to production; usage bills against the $250 first.
  - *Expiry:* Free Trial: no expiry. $250 credits: expire 6 months after claiming; not refreshed monthly; unused credit cannot be recovered or extended.
  - *Gotchas:* Trial-vs-free: 'Free Trial' (dev sandbox, no expiry, no card) is NOT the same as the 'Free Tier' (monthly free usage, pay-as-you-go). The $250 credit IS card-gated, unlike the student no-card allowance. Once credits lapse, usage beyond free tier is billed (with $5/project/month minimum). ConvoKraft is separately free of charge per Catalyst docs. The 2024 price-change notice ended the old free sandbox on 2024-03-31 and moved billing to usage-based from 2024-04-01.
  - *Source:* https://catalyst.zoho.com/catalyst-free-trial.html
- **Zoho Classes Free Edition: free for schools/teachers up to 100 students ('no questions asked')** (high) — Zoho Classes (AI-powered LMS: live streaming classes, course distribution, assignments, attendance, mobile apps) has a free edition 'completely free for all schools for up to 100 students. No questions asked' and a 'Free-For-Teacher License for up to 100 students' (per the signup page). No .edu or domain requirement is stated and no payment method is needed for the free license. Paid editions: $4 per student per year globally ('33 cents per student per month', stream live to 50,000 students); ₹250 per student per year in India. With the July 15, 2026 Classes 2.0 relaunch, paid tiers for private institutions are billed per instructor per month (Standard ~$7/instructor/month, Pro ~$20/instructor/month per press reporting; India from ~₹500/teacher/month; Zoho renders these prices dynamically and doesn't print them as text).
  - *Eligibility:* Any school or college up to 100 students (worldwide); individual teachers teaching up to 100 students. No edu-email, no accreditation proof, no credit card stated — 'no questions asked'. Larger institutions need a trial license registration or paid plan.
  - *How to claim:* Sign up at zoho.com/classes (classes signup page advertises the free-for-teacher license up to 100 students); the free edition applies automatically. Schools over 100 students register for a trial license / paid plan via the pricing page.
  - *Expiry:* No sunset date published for the 100-student free edition; 'completely free', no stated trial window.
  - *Gotchas:* The 100-student cap is on total students, so a class with 50 students in two sections consumes the whole allowance. Feature depth (AI course builder, agentic AI, lifecycle management) ships with Classes 2.0 (July 2026) and is rolling out region-by-region — India first; check your region's feature availability. Per-instructor paid pricing (2026 model) means costs scale with faculty headcount, not enrollment. Pricing figures for Standard/Pro are press-reported, not Zoho-published text — verify on the live pricing page (which blocks scrapers; direct fetch returned 403).
  - *Source:* https://www.zoho.com/classes/pricing/
- **Zoho Classes 2.0: free for ALL Central and State government schools, colleges, and universities in India** (high) — Announced July 15, 2026 with Classes 2.0: Zoho Classes is available 'at no product licensing cost to all Central and State Government schools, colleges, and universities' in India (AI LMS with embedded AI for lesson planning/grading/reporting, student lifecycle management, institutional dashboards, attendance, and support for all 22 scheduled Indian languages). Private institutions pay per-instructor subscriptions. Global rollout is 'in the coming months' with regional capabilities; the government-free offer is India-only.
  - *Eligibility:* Central and State government educational institutions in India (schools, colleges, universities). Private institutions are NOT covered (paid tiers). Individual teachers up to 100 students are covered by the separate free edition. No .edu/student-email condition; no credit card for the government free offer.
  - *How to claim:* Government institutions obtain it via Zoho's education program channels (Zoho's launch announcement and education/relationship team; larger private schools can register for trial licenses from the Classes pricing page). No self-serve public application form is published on the pages read today.
  - *Expiry:* No sunset date stated; presented as an ongoing commitment ('no product licensing cost').
  - *Gotchas:* India-only (government offer) and the 2.0 platform initially launched in India; global features/rollout pending — international government institutions should not assume coverage yet. 'No product licensing cost' excludes implementation/infrastructure costs borne by the institution. Zoho is building an education-specific LLM (Zia-based); AI features may evolve.
  - *Source:* https://www.zoho.com/news/zoho-unveils-zoho-classes-2.0-an-ai-powered-academic-lms-for-teaching-learning-and-student-success.html
- **Zoho for Startups: Zoho Wallet credits (up to ₹3,00,000 / ₹1,86,000 in India; 360-day validity) — 23 countries, DPIIT-gated in India** (medium) — Program giving startups Zoho Wallet credits to explore 55+ Zoho applications (suits, individual apps, upgrades, add-ons, renewals) plus weekday classic email support and free consultation. Credits valid 360 days from when Stage 1 is enabled; Stage 2 is an extra tranche unlocked by hosting and mapping your business domain with Zoho within 15 days of receiving Stage 1 (both tranches share the 360-day expiry, which cannot be extended). India figures (from the official Startup India resource-partner page, updated Sept 14, 2026): DPIIT-beneficiary startups (tax-exempt, National Startup Award winners, SISFS) up to ₹3,00,000 total = ₹2,00,000 for 360 days + conditional ₹1,00,000 promotional credits for 90 days (dependent on usage, non-negotiable); DPIIT-recognized startups up to ₹1,86,000 = Stage 1 ₹1,00,000 + Stage 2 ₹86,000 (domain on Zoho Mail within 15 days; note ₹1,00,000 cap on Zoho Workplace/Mail purchases). The zoho.com/startups page renders the USD amount dynamically, so it couldn't be captured by scrape today; no public funding/grants — software credits only. Credits can't buy Zoho Voice, Zoho Start, Zoho Domains, Zoho Backstage, Zoho Sign API, Campaigns PAYG, Qntrl, Vikra, TrainerCentral, most ManageEngine products, premium support, or email domains; non-transferable, non-cashable.
  - *Eligibility:* Startup must furnish a DPIIT certificate (Startup India, India) OR be associated with a startup enabler that is a registered Zoho collaborator. Must NOT be a current or past paying Zoho customer (creating a new account does not reset this); prior program beneficiaries excluded; free-trial-only usage is fine; solopreneurs/bootstrapped founders qualify if they hold the DPIIT certificate. No startup-age cap or funding cap is stated. Eligible countries (limited access, live): Australia, Austria, Bahrain, Brazil, Denmark, Egypt, France, Germany, India, Indonesia, Kenya, Malaysia, Nigeria, Norway, Saudi Arabia, South Africa, Spain, Sweden, Switzerland, Tanzania, Uganda, UAE, United Kingdom. .edu not relevant; org admin email required.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Create a Zoho account using the organization's primary/admin email. 2) Apply at zoho.com/startups (or via your Zoho-collaborator enabler; DPIIT-beneficiaries apply through the Startup India link). 3) Respond to Zoho's confirmation email; validation/credit enabling takes 5–7 business days. 4) Within 15 days of Stage 1, host and map your domain with Zoho to unlock Stage 2. 5) Spend credits in the Zoho store before the 360-day expiry.
  - *Expiry:* Credits expire 360 days from Stage 1 activation (Stage 2 shares that date); the conditional ₹1L promotional tranche (DPIIT-beneficiaries) is valid 90 days. No program-level sunset date published.
  - *Gotchas:* Exact USD credit amounts are not printed as static text on zoho.com/startups (dynamically rendered; scrape today returned gaps) — confidence in specific dollar values is therefore medium; the India-facing amounts are the firm public numbers. One-time opportunity per startup (no re-application through multiple enablers). No funding/grants. Excluded product list is long. The program is 'exclusive for startups associated or incubated with Zoho collaborators' pending broader rollout. The old 'free Zoho One for a year' subscription (previous program version) has been replaced by wallet credits.
  - *Source:* https://www.zoho.com/startups/faq.html
- **Zoho general free Standard editions (available to anyone, including students and startups)** (low) ⚠ low confidence: verify before claiming — Most Zoho apps ship a free 'Standard' edition with a small seat cap (commonly up to 3 users, more in some apps, e.g., Zoho Mail/Books/Analytics allow a few more), plus 15-day full-feature paid-plan trials. These are not education- or startup-specific offers — they are open to students and startups as individual accounts, no credit card for the free edition. Useful as a base layer (CRM, Docs, Mail, Analytics) under the Catalyst/Classes offers above.
  - *Eligibility:* Anyone (individuals, students, startups). No .edu requirement; free editions have no CC requirement in most apps (paid trials do). Seat caps vary per app.
  - *How to claim:* Sign up at zoho.com with a personal/edu/any email, start the free Standard edition of each app (or claim the 15-day paid-plan trial from the app's trial page).
  - *Expiry:* No sunset; free Standard editions are standing offers. (Not re-verified page-by-page today; per-app seat caps vary — treat 3-user as the typical default, not a guarantee.)
  - *Gotchas:* Not verified today per-app (listed at low confidence deliberately); seat limits are per-app and some require billing details to start (e.g., certain apps' trials). For startups, the Zoho for Startups credits program is the higher-value path instead of piecing together free Standard plans.
  - *Source:* https://www.zoho.com/crm/pricing.html
- **2025–26 change:** No 2025–2026 sunset of the core free offers; the changes were additive or model-level: (1) Zoho Catalyst Student Program ($250/6 months, no CC) is NEW — announced Sept 2, 2026, blogged Sept 6, 2026; nothing before it was retired. (2) Zoho for Startups: the old 'free Zoho One subscription for a year' was replaced by the wallet-credit model (FAQ explicitly calls Zoho One 'our old offering'); Stage 1/Stage 2 structure and 360-day validity are the current model. (3) Zoho Classes 2.0 relaunched July 15, 2026: pricing model flipped from per-student-per-year ($4/₹250, 2020 model) to per-instructor-per-month (Standard ~$7, Pro ~$20, press-reported) for private institutions; the 100-student free edition and the India government free-license offer were kept/strengthened — the government offer expanded explicitly to all Central & State government schools, colleges and universities at no licensing cost. (4) Catalyst historical price-change notice (effective Jan 1, 2024 / Apr 1, 2024 for pre-existing users) ended the free sandbox on Mar 31, 2024, introduced the monthly-renewing free tier and $5/project/month minimum billing on Apr 1, 2024 — still in force today, relevant gotcha for the free tier. Method note: final web-search budget was exhausted after six searches; all findings above were read today via WebFetch or same-day official-page search snapshots (zoho.com direct fetches returned 403 to the fetcher, so zoho.com pages were read via search snapshots of the live pages).
